(CHANDRA KALA NADI)

Translated by
R. SANTHANAM

DEVA BRERALADNI
(CHANDRA KALA NADI

DEVA RERALANSMI
(CHANDRA KALA NADI
BY
ACHYUTA OF BRERALA
BOOK I
(Containing 2718 Slokas)
Translated by
R. Santhanam
SAGAR PUBLICATIONS

Ved Mansion

Preface & Introduction
Salutations to Lord Ganesa, the Remover of Obstacles; to Goddess Saraswathi, the all-pervading
Mother of Learning, and to Nava Grahas, the destiny-makers of the Universe. After bringing out
Essentials of Predictive Hindu Astrology last year, 1 am happy to be amidst my readers with Book 1 of
Deva Keralam with English translation, notes etc.
Some have just heard the name of this work, Deva Keralam. Some have seen the Sanskrit
manuscript. Yet, almost every student of astrology has been keen to possess an English translation of
this work since decades. And, now, here is an occasion for all of us to actually enjoy the instructions
of Achyuta, the author of Deva Keralam.
The name itself suggests that the work belongs to Kerala, in the south of India, which is a
treasure-house of original astrological manuscripts like Jatakadesa Marga, Krishneeyam and Prasna
Marga. Each work from Kerala is a magnum opus, unto itself.
Deva Keralam has over 8200 slokas in all. Its author Achyuta had unfortunately left no clues
about his lineage, place of origin, time period etc. Lack of such information does not affect the
greatness of the work adversely. The name however suggests that it hails from Kerala.
_ The author obviously was a man of letters in astrology and had mastered various Nadi Granthas.
He specialized in use of Nadiamsa apart from employing Parasarian principles. He had consulted, in
compiling the present work, several Nadi Granthas including Surya Nadi, Chandra Nadi, Kuja Nadi,
Budha Nadi, Guru Nadi, Sukra Nadi, Sani Nadi, Lagna Nadi, Lagnadhipathi Nadi, Sarva Nadi and
Yoga Nadi
He had also quoted Garga, Vasishta, Parasara, Yavana etc. As far as I know, thc. Nadis are not
available in any form nowadays. These Nadis, unlike Saptahrshi Nadi, apparently contained principles
usable in predictive astrology and not full-fledged horoscopes. The readers may be aware, Saptharshi
Nadi contains horoscopes discussing various aspects of natives, but far less instructive principles. The
average reader can hardly know of the basis of such conclusions without the help of an experienced
tutor. However, in case of the Nadi texts consulted by our author, those apparently fulfilled, inter alia,
the sole purpose of laying down rules for exhaustive interpretation of events of numerous kinds mainly
from one starting point - Nadiamsa.
Other stages of instructions contain predicting without the help of Nadiamsa, 1.6. with the help
of special planetary combinations, positions, Ashtaka Varga, Dasa periods and the like as is normal
for any standard work we follow
What is a Nadiamsa is’. 1685014 to be explained to the reader. Most of us are us are aware of
Shodasa Vargas, the Parasarian division of a sign or Rasi into sixteen different parts of different lengths
of longitudes. What is less commonly known or employed is Nadiamsa, the 150th part of a Rasi, or one
fifth of a degree. That way, the entire space of 360 deg of the zodiac is divided into 1800 Nadiamsas.
In Table 1, in the immediately ensuing pages, I have enlisted the order and names of Nadiamsas,
as collected from the present work (the relevant original slokas appear much later in our work) and
from late C.G. Rajan of Madras, a known authority on Nadi astrology in particular. Lafe Rajan’s list

is hence taken with a specific purpose of textual utility on some occasions. And in Table 2, the
commencing and ending spaces of these 150 Nadiamsas are given.
In giving notes, I have followed the Nadiamsa divisions as shown in column 5 of Table 1. The
reader may use alternative spaces according to his choice and experience if he feels he can obtain
better effects of the text.
As could be observed, the order and names of these ‘Nadiamsas are identical for all the four
movable signs in a specified manner. Immovable signs again have a common pattern for themselves,
but different from those followed by movable and common signs. The same is the case among the four
common signs, 1.6, these have an exclusive arrangement for themselves.
For example whatever applies in this classification and identity to Aries applies equally to Cancer,
Libra and Capricorn; whatever applies to Taurus applies to Leo, Scorpio and Aquarius; and lastly
whatever applies to Gemini applies to Virgo, Sagittarius and Pisces.
The Nadiamsa count is different for movable, immovable and common signs as could be witnessed
in Table 1. In movable signs, the count is in regular order from 1 to 150 (as shown in col 1).
For an immovable sign, the count is reverse, 1.6. from 150 to 1 (i.e. 150, 149, 148, 147, 146, etc...
as shown in col.2).
For common signs the count is firstly from 76 to 150 and then from 1 to 75 (as shown in col.3).
Keeping the list for movable sign as basis. Col. 4 gives the names of these segments as per C.G.
Rajan while col. 5 gives what is at one stage followed by our present work. Table 1 will of course make
the whole thing very clear.
Now if you observe the list, Vasudha Nadiamsa is the 1st of a movable sign, the 150th of an
immovable (or fixed) sign and the 76th of a common (or dual) sign. Vaishnavi Nadiamsa is the 2nd of
a movable sign, the 149th of an immovable sign and the 77th of a common sign. Braahmi is the 3rd of
a movable sign, the 148th of an immovable sign and the 78th of acommon sign. The concerned segments
should be selected from Table 2.
Details about all the names and positions of Nadiamsas can be well understood from Table 1.
Table 2 is an easy one and can be used to know the serial number of Nadiamsa when the actual
longitude is selected following which the exact name of Nadiamsa should be referred to in Table 1.
For example there is a planet or the ascendant cusp in one of the movable signs (Aries, Cancer
Libra, Capricorn) at 10 deg 52’ of arc. That is 55th Nadiamsa which is Seethala Nadiamsa (as per col
1, Table 1). The same longitude in an immovable sign (Taurus, Leo, Scorpio, Aquarius) causes
Apavargaa Nadiamsa (as per col. 2 of Table 1), Again in a common sign (Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius,
Pisces) the relevant Nadiamsa is Raudri (as per col. 3 of Table 1). We should do well to remember that
the first Nadiamsa in a movable sign is Parameswari Nadiamsa, and in a common sign it is Mahamaaya
Nadiamsa.
For ascendant, its cusp should be used to find out its Nadiamsa position, and for the planets the
usual longitudes we obtain from the ephemeris.
In many cases, the text divides the Nadiamsa in four parts called Vipra, Kshatriya, Vaisya and
Soodra Kalas. Vipra denotes a Brahmin, Kshatriya a ruler, Vaisya a trader and Soodra the fourth caste
as per Vedic divisions. Kala means time. Simply these are quarters 1 to 4 (each of 3’ longitude) of a
given Nadiamsa and are superior or inferior in that order for certain purposes. In other words they
(40 not always indicate the birth in a certain caste as prevailing in olden days. These are actually meant
for attainment of perfection in prediction. Needless to mention, this kind of division makes the whole

zodiac into 7200 equal minor segments, each of 3’ arc (total 21600’ accounting for 360deg) - 1800 each
of Vipra, Kshatriya, Vaisya and Soodra Kalas, the use of which can hardly allow accuracy to escape
from the hands of a master astrologer.
The reader going through the pages will find the extent of changes in events each quarter (viz.
Vipra etc.) of a Nadiamsa can introduce. In the same Nadiamsa, for example Vipra Kala gives certain
result while another of the 3 parts gives completely different results.
Another division made of this Nadiamsa is first half and second half making the whole zodiac in
3600 parts. That is of 6’ longitude. Here again the effects are absolutely in mutual variance.
On quite limited occasions, we find that a Nadiamsa is made into six parts attributing to Garga’s
authority. Meseems, this division is however questionable and must have been wrongly attributed to
the said sage.
True, exact degree of ascendant in terms of degrees and minutes of arc is very essential to use
some of the instructions contained hereof. Then without that, there can be no real astrology. Astrology
of approximation can only be disastrous. To use the principles containing Nadiamsas, the astrologer
must also obtain excellence in standard methods of rectification, prenatal epoch theory and through
Horary methods. Once that is done, with known events the correct Nadiamsa can be located.
For an established astrologer, Ayanamsa or precession is not an unsettled issue. Let me however
state for the benefit of the new reader that Lahiri’s Chaitrapaksha Ayanamsa is the accurate one when
the horoscope is cast using the exact coordinates at every point.
Not just for Nadiamsa, accuracy of Ayanamsa is a must even in other cases, like Navamsa,
Sapthamsa and Shashtiamsa. To wit, if a planet, say Saturn is in Aries 19deg 59’ as per Lahiri ayanamsa,
another ayanamsa even with a small difference of minus 2’ of arc can place Saturn somewhat ahead
and give it Libra Navamsa instead of Virgo navamsa due to Lahiri. Saturn even in Aries Rasi with
Libra Navamsa protects one from heart ailments, gives him longevity and helps him progress in his
calling. The same Saturn with Aries sign position but in Virgo Navasma makes him wholly unreliable,
and makes him a victim of some sort of diseases of hand and legs. This difference could be easily located
by an experienced astrologér. Same is the case with other divisions including Nadiamsa.
All the rules given in this book do not, as could be seen, always revolve around Nadiamsa. And
each Nadiamsa in each sign is of course not given in the context of delineation. Select Nadiamsas are
given and they can be easily applied to the same kind of sign - movable, immovable or common, thus
multiplying their use four - fold. Sometimes the reader should be careful for they are just for a given
sign only.
Some Nadiamsas appear with synonym though not in that name. Some have not been enlisted in
both the lists of the text in which case, I have to the possible extent supplied suitable alternatives. These
may or may not be acceptable to the reader.
There are umpteen rules given by the text without ever considering Nadiamsa division. Such
rules, which can be rarely found in other sources than the present text, could be of exemplary use. For
an easy understanding several groups of slokas have been given suitable captions and any reader can
understand where a Nadiamsa features, and where it does not.
The beauty of the text lies in the fact that based on one planetary hint, many of the native’s relatives
have been exhaustively discussed.
The original suffers from certain defects. In some cases, the Sanskrit heading denoting a certain
Nadiamsa and ascendant clashes with the actual text thereof. The reader better follow the body text

for details. In some cases, copying errors, hopeless interpolations etc. are there, particularly in terms
of transit, using meaninglessly words like "Sphuta Yoga" even though only one planet features. In
some cases, the expression "Sukhi" is used denoting happiness although the effects are not always so.
Such misnomers including some corrupt words have been as far as possible, if not invariably but
carefully, omitted in translation to protect to the reader from being misguided. This mention is not
belittle the author, but with an understanding mind of the limitations of the copyist.
The Contents pages given before the commencement of the text, for understandable reasons, is
only brief as it is bound to be. For in each section, a number of items have been discussed and enlisting
them is only enlarging the volume.
I have in fact tried to give due exposure to the original’s true spirit and I believe I have succeeded
to a great extent in this direction.
I have left no stone unturned in fulfilling my task as the reader expects, having provided English
translations for rare standard works like Saravali (2 vols), Parasara Hora (vol 1 only), Suka Nadi, Hora
Sara etc.... etc... Yet there ought to be some shortcoming out of my ignorance, what in Sanskrit, and
what in astrology. In such an event, I can only pray the reader’s broad-minded favour of forgiving me
on such count of failures.
I have however the happiness that I am the first person to have undertaken the task of rendering
these slokas in English and the reader is well aware that so far no language translation has been
provided for the present original text. The present issue, Book 1, contains 2718 slokas while Book 2
and Book 3 containing discussions on further Nadiamsas in many ascendants, and a number of general
rules independent of Nadiamsas, will soon follow to complete the work in question.
The text also excels in suggesting suitable remedies for many shortcomings in the lives of the native
and his relatives.
J am specially appreciative of the interest and enthusiasm shown by Mr. Narinder Kumar Sagar
in placing the present title before the readers.
I will feel greatly honoured if the reader finds something useful in my present efforts.
र. Santhanam,
Editor,
The Times Of Astrology,
9/1040 Govindpuri,
Kalkaji,
New Delhi 19 (India)
January 15, 1992 Telephone 6462777

मतंगयोगजातस्य स्वभार्खसंगमेष्यति।

AGS व् तु ara वा मृतिमादिशेत्।
सुखधिपं नीचनक्रनवाशगे। । ९। ।

दुभगावसरे जातत ॥ wi | १०। |
भगिन्येका चिरायुष्य
Sor चन्द्रे भगिन्येका च भग । । ११।।
च कट्टत्रद्रसवान् पित।
त्रयश्चेति स्वपिता बहूभाग्यवान्।। १२।।
AMY संचारं क्रयविक्रयजीवनम्।
एत्धन्यसवस्त्रदिनानाव्यपारमादिशेत्। । १३। ।
वृद्धिजीवन वाणिन्यं स्वपिता ्रातृभत्रिमान्।
8-131/2 The Matanga Yoga subject will
desire union with his spouse (1.6. he will not join
others). His spouse will become a widow due to
separation and will die. The native’s father will
die in (native’s) childhood or before native’s marriage. If the Moon placed in Srikaara Nadi Amsa,
becomes the lord of the 4th, (or) occupying Scorpio/Capricorn Navamsa, and is aspected by the
9th lord (Jupiter), one born in Durbhaga Nadi
Amsa will have no brothers born after him. His
only sister will be long-lived. So also the mother.
If the Moon is in Durbhaga Nadiamsa, there will
be one sister who will be unfortunate. There will
be also destruction of step-brothers while father
will have two marriages. After the native attains
15th year of age, the father will become very
fortunate. He father) will tour many countries
for business in diamonds, grains, clothes etc. He
will live kv commercial activities, be an intelléctual and will have brothers and sisters.
Notes: Sloka 9 and others deal with Aries
ascendant in which the birth Nadiamsa should be
Durbhaga (from 7 deg. 12’ to 7 deg. 24’). Then
birth Navamsa falls in Gemini. Another space for
Durbhaga Nadiamsa in Aries is from 11 deg, 48’
to 12 deg. (Cancer Navamsa) The Moon should
be in aspect to 9th lord Jupiter. She should be in
Srikaara Nadiamsa (or) be in Scorpio or
Capricorn Navamsa. Then the native will have no
younger brother, but one younger sister who
along with mother will be long-lived.
Srikaara Nadiamsa is mentioned as
Karikaraa Nadiamsa 107 the Moon to be in, (with
other conditions as per out text) by C.G. Rajan.
Karikaara Nadiamsa is from 6 deg. 24 to 6 deg.
36 of a movable sign, 23 deg. 24 to 23 deg. 36’ of
an immovable Rasi and 21 deg. 24 to 21 deg. to
36’ of acommon sign. Hence Srikaara Nadiamsa
of our text is Karikaara Nadiamsa, as per Table of
Nadiamsas, supra.
Even though the 4th lord Moon is in debility
in Navamsa, or in the 8th in Capricorn in Navamsa, she will give a long lease of life to the mother,
because of Jupiter’s aspect. The aspect as a rule
should be seen in Rasi chart only. Not in Navamsa and other charts. For aspects emanate only by:
longitudinal distances.
Coborns come in the picture because the
Moon is a death-dealer, being lord of 2nd from
3rd.
The sister will be unfortunate if the said
Moon is in Durbhaga Nadiamsa. Durbhaga
means unfortunate. Instead of acquiring carly
death, she will live an unfortunate life. Durbhaga
Nadiamsa = 7 deg. 00’ to 7 deg. 12’ in a movable
Rasi, 22 deg 48’ to 23 deg. 00’ of an immovable
Rasi, and 2 deg. 00’ to 2 deg 12’ of a commun
Rasi. This position also causes loss of stepbrothers, This Nadiamsa repeats from 11 deg.
48’-12 deg. 0’ of a movable sign, from 18 dey, 0’ -
18 deg. 12’ of an immovable sign and from 2h deg.
48’-27 deg. of a common sign.

14-16. Father’s destruction: Destruction of
father will result if Saturn occupies the degree
cause 4 by adding the longitudes of the Sun (Sura
= Su ) and the 4th lord. For one born in the first
half of Maalini Nadi Amsa with Mathanga Yoga,
the first sub-period of Janma Dasa (first Dasa)
will prove ominous for father bringing illness or
death.

Deva Keralam 3
Notes: Suramsa also means Sura Nadiamsa.
Seeing the words ‘Tattrikonage’ in sloka 14,
‘Suramsage’ ibid is not translated as Sura
Nadiamsa while it could also mean so. Sura
means Sun and hence it denotes degree occupied
by the Sun at birth. This should be added to that
of the 4th lord.
As stressed by me often in my writings the
4th lord denotes father’s death while the Sth lord
denotes mother’s death. This technique is used
widely by many Nadi Granthas. In my own
studies, I did find these clues working quite effectively.
Sloka 14 gives important clue about father’s
death. Saturn should not be in the degree arrived
at by adding the degree of the 4th lord (father’s
death-dealer) and of the Sun (signifying father).
If so, being a dire enemy of the Sun, and a planet
incharge of death, Saturn will cause early destruction of father.
From this, we can form another rule of our
own. The 5th lord rules mother’s death. The
Moon rules the mother. If Saturn occupies the
degree denoted by the degree arrived at by adding the longitudes of the Moon and Sth lord,
early loss of mother can be anticipated.
In the absence of Saturn being there, the
transits of malefics like Saturn/Rahu/Ketu/Mars
will cause some physical adversity to the parent
concerned. Not death always.

17-18. Dasa effects: In the first Dasa (Janma
Dasa), there will be some comforts and investiture of sacred thread. In the sub- period of 10th
lord in the Vipat Dasa (third from birth) there will
be marriage or in the 22nd or 25th year? There
will be obstacles in childhood for one born in
Maatanga Yoga.
Notes: Terms like Janma Dasa occur often
in this work. These details could be found in the
following table:
Ist dasa : Janma dasa
2nd dasa : Sampath dasa
3rd dasa : Vipat Dasa
4th dasa : Kshema dasa
Sth-dasa : Prathyak dasa
6th dasa : Sadhaka dasa
7th dasa : Vadha (Nidhana) dasa
8th dasa : Maitra dasa
Oth dasa- : Parama Maitra dasa
A general yardstick is: odd dasas from birth
are inauspicious while even dasas are auspicious.

6 Chandra Kala Nadi

caused by benefics being in 5th, 6th and 7th identical with own, friendly or exaltation signs while

own/exaltation places. Results: Kingship, fame,
courage, happinesss and leadership. Also see
Sloka 46 infra.

44, Danger to coborn: The native’s coborns
will be struck by destruction (or dangcr) when
Saturn reaches the Rasis, or trines of the undermentioned planets: (1) Navamsa dispositor of
11th lord.(2) Navamsa dispositor of 6th lord.
Notes: Navamsa dispositor is given high importance in prediction by various Nad/ texts.
Navamsa dispositor is the owner of the Navamsa
a planet is in. Example: Mercury is in Libra
Navamsa. Then his Navamsa dispositor is Venus.
The present sloka asks us to note the
Navamsa lords of the 11th lord and 6th lord.
Then we have to find out in which Rasi, such
dispositors are placed. Saturn coming over to
such Rasi/trine thereof will prove dangerous to
one’s brothers/sisters.
The 11th lord is understandable. Why the
6th lord comes in the picture can be understood
by some probe. The 6th is 8th from 11th.
in my view, this rule applies only to elder
coborn as the 11th, and the 8th from 11th feature.
To apply this to younger coborns ‘one should be
somewhat careful. But the 11th lord has been
cited regarding younger coborn also.

47-48. Cancer Ascendant: When the Moon
is the lord of the ascendant, the sub-period of
Jupiter in the dasa of Mars will cause extreme
difficulties, untimely death, evils to one’s own
people, and destruction of female children. In the
absence of these, financial destruction will take
place in the end of the dasa (of Mars).
Notes: These two slokas relate to Cancer
ascendant, as the Moon is required to rule the

Deva Keralam
ascendant. The dasa of Mars (lord of Sth and
10th) and the sub period of Jupiter (ruling 6th and
9th) is quite destructive for the native in the form
of loss of daughters, many difficulties to the native
and his kinsmen. He may also have fear of untimely death because the combination of Mars -
Jupiter does not seem to be a good augury in
general.
Also make a note of Jupiter’s period being
evil for one born with Cancer Navamsa, in Aries
ascendant. This need not be straightaway applied
to birth in any of the other eight Navamsas of
Aries ascendant.

54-55. Abala Nadiamsa - Firsthalf: One
born in this part will be a Brahmin, be happy, be

Notes: Slokas 54 to 93 deal with an immovable ascendant including Taurus. The reader will
suitably according to context understand these.
Abala Nadiamsa is also known as Prabha
Nadiamsa according to late C.G. Rajan.

‘ Chandra Kala Nadi
This Nadiamsu in an immovable Rasi extends from 26 deg. 48’ to 27 deg. 00’ of urc. And
can cover even the 9th Navamsa Virgo according
to a given case. In case of movable sign. this
Nadiamsa extends from 3 deg. to 3 deg. 12’ and in
a common sign from 18 deg. to 18 deg. 12’ of
space.
The first half of this Nadiamsa in this case is
26 deg. 48’ to 26 deg. 54 of arc, again with a
possibility of attaining cither Leo Navamsa or
Virgo Navamsa.
The effects given in the current slokas are
for just birth in first half of Abala Nadiamsa,
wilhout reference to positions of any planets.

56. Coborns: Mars in ascendant (which is an
immovable sign) while Jupiter is in the 5th house
will deprive the native of after- borns. Even if
there be such a birth, only death (of alter-born)
will result.
Notes: Immovable signs are Taurus, Leo,
Scorpio and Aquarius. For Taurus and Aquarius
ascendants, this rule can be safely applicd as
neither Mars nor Jupiter will be in own sign. In
case of Leo, Jupiter in 5th will be in Sagittarius,
and in case of Scorpio both Mars and Jupiter will
be in own signs (Scorpio and Pisces respectively).
Hence, for Leo ascendant and Scorpio ascendant, this rule should be used after a thorough
scrutiny, more so for Scorpio ascendant.

88-92. Sixth Lord: For effects of Abala
Nadiamsa, other sources may also be consulted.
If the Moon is in Nirmala Nadiamsa as the 10th
lord is in his own Rasi/Navamsa, the native will
have knowledge of Vedanta (second and most
important part of Mimamsa or third of the three
greal divisions of Hindu philosophy) and Tatwas
(24 in number) and will perform actions enjoined
by the Vedas. He will be ever-pious. He will be
pure, virtuous, eloquent in speech and will worship Lord Vishnu and Lord Siva. He will leave his
birth country in a revolution. He will incur danger
after his 10th year or 12th year. He will wander in
many coutries. Due to destruction of parents, he
will incur abundant grief. He will live in a town on
the banks of the Mahanadi. One born in Abala
Nadiamsa will enjoy yoga (i.e. effects due to for11111816 birth), and will be quite happy. His fortune
will be on the increase in the beginning and end
of 3rd dasa.
Notes: From hereon, the relative slokas deal
with Aquarius ascendant, an immovable sign.
The Moon is the 6th lord who should be in Nirmala Nadiamsa. The lord of 10th Mars should be
in Aries or Scorpio, either in Rasi or in Navamsa.
Then the native will be skilful in Vedanta and the
like. Other effects may also be noted.
The beginning and concluding parts of 3rd
dasa causing prosperity suggests that the middle
portion will be adverse.
Maha Nadi can be interpreted to be the river
so named, or a great river like the Ganges or
Cauvery.

beget progeny. Other authorities quote Maharshi
Vyasa suggesting donation of the image of Lord
Krishna (by the’ said relative to overcome such
evils). The idol of Santhana Gopala (Krishna) be

130-131. About Mother: When the birth is in
the former half of Agada Nadiamsa, as the Moon
is positioned in Kutila Nadiamsa, following effects may be noticed. Sins will be galore in the
mother’s family. The mother of the native will be
fond of promoting quarrels. There will be many
troubles to the mother’s sister. The said maternal
aunt will be of questionable character and will
indulge in sinful acts.
Notes: Kutila Nadiamsa is: from 2 deg. 48’
to 3 deg. 0’ of a movable sign, from 27 deg. 0’ to
27 deg. 12’ of an immovable sign, and from 17 deg.
48’ to 18 deg. 0’ of acommon sign. The Moon in
one of these three position, while birth is in first
half of Agada Nadiamsa in Aquarius, the results
described in the slokas above will come to pass.

132-135. According to Chandra Nadi: The
Moon being the lord of the house signifying
maternal relatives (i.e. in 6th house, the ascendant
being Aquarius) if placed in Kutila Nadiamsa, in
aspect to Mercury gives a long lifespan to maternal relatives. According to some authorities,
(early) destruction will take place. The mother of
such a native will be of questionable nature, and
will incur troubles on account of brotheis and be
emaciated. She will incur widowhood and also
enmity of step-children, during the native’s
childhood. She will beget auspicious effects fora
long time. (This part of the text is a corrupt
addition, considering other bad effects, particularly early widowhood.) The mother will,
however, be endowed with along lease of life. She
will beget happiness through her middle
daughter. These results are declared in Chandra
Nadi, Effects - good and bad - about mother and
maternal relatives have elaborately been dealt
with in Sukra Nadi,
Notes: In slokas 130 and 131, evilelfects are
described for the mother, maternal aunts etc. for
the Moon being in Kutila Nadiamsa. According
to the present slokas, Mercury (the significator of
maternal relatives) getting connected with the
Moon gives a long-life to maternal uncle. There
is another source to specify early end. Long life
to maternal relatives, as per Chandra Nadi is
apparently acceptable. This yoga is however adverse for the mother.

139-142. Half sister: If the Moon be in Kutila
Nadiamsa, there is a possibility of having twin
half-sisters. The half sister will incur much grief
on account of destruction of her progeny. Her
husband will be pained by diseases and will be
bereft of coborn. The half sister will in her midlife be deprived of fortunes and will suffer penury.
Her husband will earn {ume through royal service.
Their fortunes will recover after some interval.
They will prosper through children, when the
native will be in his 2nd dasa. The brother-in-law
in question will acquire men-borne conveyances,
fame and yoga effects (1.6. prosperity) and be
happy.

154-1571/2 . The Sun devoid of beneficial
aspect, in the Sth from 6th house along with Ketu
will be an obstacle for step-brother to beget
progeny. He will lose wealth on account of
Soodras and will later on be happy. When the
native is in his 8th/10th year of age, his father will
incur destruction (losses) on account of Soodras.
Afterwards he will obtain great happiness which
will lead to amiability with step brothers. Prior to
this, the native’s step-brother-in-law will enjoy
effects of good yoga (prosperity).
Note: The sequence of these hints can be
arranged thus to have a clear picture:
The Sun with Ketu in the 10th house without
a benefic’s aspect leads to the following:
1. Step-brother will have difficulties in
begetting progeny. He will initialy lose wealth on
account of Sudras and then will lead a happy life.
2. When the native will be 8 or 10 years of
age, financial losses to father will have to be
feared. After sometime he will have good terms
with children born to his other wife (than the
native’s mother) and be happy.

Note: Here we need some clarifications.
Seeing the sequence after sloka 226, this sloka
does not scem to relate to Mars’ position in Mangala Nadiamsa. This apparently relates to
Varuna Nadiamsa. Actually Mangala Nadiamsa
slokas are wrongly appended hereof, while the

line having been placed in brackets. This is a
misplaced line. In the 2nd line, the reference is to
some planet in {116 to 6th lord’s Navamsa position, or with reference to Lagna Navamsa (vide
3rd line). To which planet the reference is made,
is not understood. Hence these 3 - line sloka may
be treated as defective. Yet, rendering in English
has been made as above.

one Ayana to one year, which is 11/2 years).

Sun’s dasa) being quite favourable in respect of
father’s prosperity.

चक्रयेगे धनुर्लग्ने तदीशे वारूणंशगे।
भरगयाधिपे कुमाराशे भाग्यवांश्च न संशयः। | २४४। |
244. Ascendant being in Sagittarius, with
Chakra Yoga while the lord of ascendant, Jupiter
is in Varuna Nadiamsa, and the 9th lord (Sun) is

Deva Keralam an
in Kumara Nadiamsa, will make the native fortunate.
Notes: Reference to Sagittarius native
having Chakra Yoga, along with two more conditions are made in the original. The result of these
three conditions is mere fortune, while Chakra
Yoga alone can give the most excellent result of
emperorship for any Lagna.
For the information of the reader, Chakra
Yoga is formed thus: when the six alternative
houses from Lagna are occupied by the 7 planets
from the Sun to Saturn, this Yoga is formed. As a
result, the native will "become the most powerful
emperor at whose feet, the heads of many kings
will lay in honour”. In the face of this effect,
Chakra Yoga in the present sloka, with two other
conditions, giving mere fortunes is of doubtful
origin.
This anamoly is extended to Slokas 250 etc.
infra. In my opinion, without Chakra Yoga, these
verses be taken.
Kumara Nadiamsa is not found anywhere.
It is Kumari that is enlisted in our work which
extends from 21 deg. 12’ to 21 deg. 24’ of a movable sign, from 8 deg. 36’ to 8 deg. 48’ of an
immovable sign and from 6 deg. 12’ to 6 deg, 24’
of a common sign.

245-2461/2. Effects for combinations in
sloka 244 - continued: The native’s father will also
incur a high degree of prosperity. The subject will

Sagittarius native by virtue of her ownership of the
8th house, unless she is connected with Mars, the
Sth lord.

263-264. Wide rulership: Jupiter in the ascendant, Venus in the 4th, and a malefic in the 3rd
house - these 3 planets so placed will make one a
king’s counsellor, well-versed in royal administration and aruler of manya country. He will become
famous and be learned in ancient scriptures. He
will be a devotee of Lord Siva and perform daily
rituals to Him.

265-267. Sun in Scorpio: The Sun in Kumara
Nadiamsa and Rahn in Scorpio will make a Sagittarius native a devotee of Lords Vishnu and Siva.
He will always follow a virtuous path and his
conduct will conform to Vedic codes. He will be
learned in ancient Sastras, be religious, and endowed with wealth. His father will also have
similar qualities. The native will be famous among
his men.

268-270. If Saturn is the 9th lord and is in
Capricorn Navamsa, the native will be inwardly
given to anger, be (outwardly) pleased while his
father, will be a miser, helpful to others, be somewhat talkative and religiously disposed. The
native’s paternal uncle will be a great sinner while
another paternal uncle will be meritorious. The
native’s paternal aunt will live a long married life,
will have many sons and be happy. She will

271-272. According to Senior Garga: With
Jupiter in Varuna Nadiamsa while the Sun is in
Kumara Nadiamsa, the native’s father will have a
brother who will have, no doubt, children. He will
have two barren wives, one giving birth to dead
children. After a long interval, progeny will be
born {to one of the wives).

273. Limited progeny: The 5th lord in Mangala Nadiamsa in association with the Sun and
Mercury will restrict the number of issues (to the
native).

274-276. Dead children: One एग रा Varuna
Nadiamsa having the Moon in the 3rd house will
beget many unsurviving children. One of the
children will (however) be long-lived. The native
of Chakra Yoga having birth in Sagittarius Lagna
while Jupiter is in Varuna Nadiamsa will have two
wives and obtain progeny. The native of Kumara
Nadiamsa will have two paternal uncles endowed
with sons. The paternal uncle will have progeny
after a long interval. (2 lines after sloka 276 have
some mix up).

277-283. Note: These Slokas are repetition
of Sloka No.271 to 276 + two lines before Sloka
277, supra. Hence these are not provided with

एकोनविंशवर्षदिविपद्दाये सुपुत्रवान्।

284, In the 19th year (or) in 3rd Dasa, birth
of a (surviving) child is denoted. Because of
Saturn (i.e.Moon) in the 3rd, first child will pass
away (early).
धनांशगे तत्तिकोणे स्फ्टयोगं गते शनौ।
धनेशपृष्ठराशै तु स्वस्य भ्रगयमहद्विपत्। । २८५। |
285, Loss of fortunes: For birth in Varuna
Nadiamsa, Saturn in transit in the Rasi denoted
by the natal Navamsa of the 2nd lord or its trine
will cause loss of fortunes. This transit of Saturn
could also be in a Rasi aspected by the 2nd lord
at birth.
Notes: Saturn himself is the 2nd lord for
Sagittarius ascendant. We have to see in which
Navamsa Saturn is placed at birth, When Saturn
himself transits such a Rasi or 2 trine thereof, the
native will have decline of fortunes. Similar use
of Saturn’s transit be made with reference to the
Rasi he occupies at birth.
भाग्येशे सप्तमे वा द्रादशंशदिजन्मति।
स्फुटयोगं गते मन्दे स्वमतिक्लेशमाप्नुयात्। । २८६। |
286. Mental agony: When Saturn in transit
comes to the 7th Rasi counted from the Navamsa
Rasi occupied by the 9th lord at birth, or the 7th
from Dwadasamsa Lagna (should be from 12th
lord’s Navamsa), mental agony to father will come
to pass.
भग्याधिपदशकाले Tat ललने शनौ।
सौम्यस्यान्तर्दशपाके स्वदेशे नृपतेर्मुतिः। । २८७। |
287. King’s death (2): The native’s ruler will
die with Saturn in movement in Virgo during (the
native’s) 9th lord’s Dasa, in a sub-period of
benefic (or Mercury).
स्वमात जनकश्चापि yaa नात्र संशयः।

288-290. In the periods just mentioned (vide
sloka 287), there may be death of maternal grandfather. In the end of first Dasa, with the Sun and
Saturn moving in the end of Pisces or in Taurus,
the king will meet with untimely death(?). For the
native of Varuna Nadiamsa when Saturn moves in
the end of Scorpio, death of King...... ifhe survives
destruction of kingdom will occur. Big quarrels
with relatives will also take place.
Note: The above 3 slokas have got some
confusing mix-up, serving no clear hints.
सुतेशंशे fare 1 १
291. Royal Service and Association: If the
Sth lord’s Navamsa position is identical with a
trine from natal ascendant it will make the native
serve a King. The 9th lord’s Navamsa position, as
above, will enable the native move in association
with kings.
Notes: Trines from Sagittarius are Aries
and Leo. If Mars is in Aries Navamsa or in Leo
Navamsa, for a birth in Sagittarius ascendant, it
will enable the native serve a king. Such a deduction be made also with reference to the Navamsa
occupied by the Sun at birth.

292-2921/2. The sign that is trinal with retference to the Navamsa occupied by the 9th lord
at birth will be the Janma Rasi (Moon sign) of the
native’s father. Should the 10th lord be in Mangala Nadiamsa while there is a conjunction of the
said planet with the lord of the 9th, the native will
in the Dasa of the 10th lord enjoy great fortunes.
Yet in the sub-period of Mercury (in the Dasa of
10th lord), enmity (with others) will prevail.

8th lord and in the sub-period of the 10 lord, one
will incur sorrow through co- born, and he will be
worried on account of severe illness to his spouse
and will have sorrow on account of the king.
These results depend on the strength (and weakness) of the ‘9th lord.

297-2971/2 . Trouble to spouse: At a time
when Saturn (in transit) aspects Leo (or) when he
transits the end of Sagittarius (or) the sign
Capricorn, the subject’s spouse will incur physical
illnesses, and there will be great evils to her family

लभेशंशे तत्तरिकोणे स्फुटयेगं गते शनौ। । २९८। I

298-299 1/2. Mother: There will be evils to
the mother of Varuna Nadiamsa native when
Saturn transits the Rasi identical with the Navamsa position of the 11th lord or its trine. Also when
Saturn comes to the sign that is aspected by the
11th lord. Diseases and physical danger to mother
will have to be feared in the concluding part of
Vipath (3rd) Dasa, or in the first sub-period of
Kshema (4th) Dasa.
Notes: Transit Saturn opposing natal Venus,
or moving in such a Rasi/trine equal to the
Navamsa occupied by Venus at birth endangers
the native’s mother. Apparently for Sagittarius
ascendant, Venus holds some clue about death of
mother. (So also the 5th lord Mars).
The 11th house playing a role in this regard
can be seen in the following slokas.
सुखेशे वारूणंशस्थे चपे कन्यांशगे शनौ। । 3001 |
लाभे शनीक्िते काले मातृनिर्यणमादिशेत्।
मेषे वृषभे मन्दे गोच
सूर्यशि तत्त्रिकोणे वा पितनिर्याणमादिशेत्।
300-301 1/2. Parents: The 4th lord in Varuna
Nadiamsa while Saturn is placed at birth in Sagittarius Rasi and Virgo Navamsa - in this case
predict mother’s death when Saturn in transit
comes to aspect the 11th house. Instead of Virgo
Navamsa, if Saturn is in Aries Navamsa, his movements in Taurus will bring about father’s end. This
can as well occur in the 4th Dasa (Kshema). The
death of father may alternatively occur when
Saturn transits the Navamsa Rasi of the natal Sun
or its trine.
Notes: The 4th lord Jupiter in Varuna
Nadiamsa, while Saturn is in Sagittarius 16 deg.
40’ to 20 deg. (Virgo Navamsa), the aspect of
Saturn on the 11th (from Capricorn/Aries/Leo)
will endanger the mother. Instead if Saturn is
within 3 deg. 20° of Sagittarius (Aries Navamsa),
his transit in Taurus will endanger father.

He शनीकषिते काले स्वपिता यास्यति व्यथाम्।

303-304 1/2. In the Dasa of the 8th lord, or in
the beginning/concluding phase of 9th lord's
Dasa, if Saturn aspects the sign Scorpio, the
native’s father will face mental agony, and fear of
untimely death. Suitable remedies be done (to
overceome these evils). The native during the said
transit will get effects of great Raja yoga.
Notes: From sloka 301 we gather that
Saturn’s transit in Taurus will be bad for father.
From sloka 304, we find that Scorpio being
aspected by Saturn (from Aquarius/
Taurus/Virgo) will again prove bad for father.
These two clues can be profitably used in case of
Sagittarius ascendant according to circumstances.
Untimely death can also turn into some
dangerous illness.

305-307. Evils to the native’s children be
foretold when Saturn in transit reaches the Rasi
identical with 12th lord’s natal Navamsa, or its
trine, or a Rasi that is aspected by 12th lord at
birth. In the absence of the said effect, Saturn
moving in such a Rasi will cause destruction of the
ruler of the nativ’s country(?) Further infantile
mortality and diseases to children belonging to
maternal relatives will come to pass.

Notes: In the first line, the expression
‘Becjayugmaanu- saarathah’ should be
‘Ojhayugmaanusaarathah’ to make proper sense.
The author suggests that each Rasi dasa should
be made into sub periods spreading over twelve
7odiacal signs according to odd and even signs.
For odd signs, the counting is in the reverse orders
while for even signs, the counting is in usual asccnding order. But there is no clear instruction
as to what particular Jaimini dasa is referred to
Apparently, this sloka is a lone occupant of this
part of the text.

345-3451/2 . The native will get auspicious

- The house aspected by the lord of the ascendant
will prove favourable. This rule is generally found
in many standard texts. But in this text we find
some additional instructions about 9th and 10th
lords. The house aspected by such a lord, or the
house occupied by such a lord, will prove auspicious. One of these lords for example related to
the 2nd house as above will give wealth, happy
family life and the like. To the 3rd - acquisition of
coborn, good terms with them etc. To the 4th -
education, conveyances etc. To the 500 - progeny,
intelligence etc. To the 6th - freedom from diseases, debts etc. only when the planet in question
is well-placed. To'the 7th - auspicious marriage,
chaste sex life etc. To the 8th - the 9ta lord will
confer fortunes if strong; misfortunes/ professional adversities if weak. To the 12th - success in
foreign countries by 9th lord if strong and professional progress if 10th lord is strong. As far as
ascendant is concerned, such relation by lord of
9/10th is a Raja Yoga.

348-350. In the malefic dasa cited earlier

become more fortunate.

Jupiter if in transit aspects Sagittarius during the
dasa of Sun/Moon/Mars/ Jupiter, the subject will
obtain better results.

4th house
5th house
6th house
With the help of above table, we can find
easily the Navamsa dispositor of the lord of the
house noted in column 3. Then we can link it to
Saturn’s transit. An inquisitive reader can take a
great advantage of these clues. Also, see slokas
526-527 infra.
Further to an intelligent student, the above
table will clarify, why the 9th house is treated as
house of father instead of 10th which is 7th from
4th.

355-356. The native of Varuna Nadiamsa
will prosper after 35 years of age. He will obtain
importance apart from elephants, wealth etc. He
will be honoured by the king and will be victorious
over a multitude of enemies. He will earn a great
deal of honour and fame.

357-359, Should Rahu and the Sun be in
Aquarius, while the Sun begets a Navamsa ruled
by 7th lord (i.e. ruled by Mercury), the subject will
have union with other women, that is, he will be
adulterous. Further he will suffer from windy and
urinary disorders. After some interval he will
emerge pure-hearted and will give up the habit of
adultery. He will ever take to bad path and be
somewhat troubled by diseases. His wife will be
perennially ill, but will intermittently recover. If
the Sun is in a Navamsa of Venus (while being in
Aquarius Rasi in association with Rahu), the native will enjoy abundant prosperity. He will be
happy through spouse (i.e. she will be devoid of
health problems).
Notes: In the above yoga, Sun in Mercury's
Navamsa will be a source of health problems
while in Venusian Navamsa he will give her good
health.

373-374. Umbilical cord: If the 10th house
is aspected by Jupiter while the Moon is with
Rahu in the first half of Uraga Nadiamsa in Scorpio, the child born will be entangled with umbilical cord. If the birth is in the 2nd half, there will
be no such entanglement. Jupiter aspecting the
birth in former half of this Nadiamsa will at times
give happiness (at the time of delivery, free from
such complications).

375-378 1/2. Other effects: Birth in second
half of Uraga Nadiamsa will eliminate fear of
extinction of the family. In addition, the subject is
born in a city ruled by Mlcchchas, in a region of
river. If the birth is in first half of this Nadiamsa,
it will be in a pilgrim center in a colony of Brahmins, Other authorities state that the birth will be
in a place specially devoted to Lord Siva. The
native mentioned above will be a lonely child of
the family whose after-borns will remain
destroyed. One born in the second half of this
Nadiamsa will be blessed with brothers and
sisters. He will lose his father in childhood. Three
of his elder sons will be happy. One born in first
half of this Nadiamsa will have one elder sister
who will be happy. He will have various business
activities. His daughter will be devoted to her
husband.

381-381 1/2. First Half of Uraga Nadiamsa:
Birth in this half will easure a (long) living father.
The native’s mother will be (often) troubled by
diseases. His 3rd dasa will cause her death, with
Saturn moving in the end of Libra, or in Scorpio.
Notes: In Cancer, the first half of this
Nadiamsa extends from 29 deg. 24’ to 29 deg. 30’
of arc. The second half extends from 29 deg. 30’
to 29 deg. to 36’. Burth in first half will give a
long-living father while the 2nd half will cause
danger to the father during the native’s 8th/10th
year of age. Also see sloka 396 infra.
The first-half native will not however be
happy about father, nor will he inherit paternal
property (vide slokas 388-390 infra).

388-390. Family, wealth etc.: One born in
the second half of this Nadiamsa will indulge in
thieving. He will be addicted to others’ women.
Otherwise he will get a wicked spouse promoting
quarrels. The native of the former half will have a
good family but be bereft of happiness through
father. He will have self- earned wealth and be
fond of parents. Rahu placed in Arudra Nakshatra will give wealth, valour and gratefulness. If
Rahu is in Navamsa (or divisions) of a malefic, the
native will be base and be a sinner. He will cat
anything and be ever troubled by diseases.

391-393. Rahu: The native of Uraga
Nadiamsa with Rahu in the 4th quarter of Arudra
(i.e. in Pisces Navamsa) will be fond of friends.
One born in the first half of this Nadiamsa will be
a Vaisya (trader) and will have wealth gathered by
just means. if the birth be in the second half, the
subject’s wealth will be by unjust-means. He will
be in the service of Mlechchas and will have
abundant business activities. He will have three
elder brothers. Sometimes birth in second half of
this Nadiamsa will give two marriages. One born
in the first half of the said Nadiamsa will have one
sister who will be auspicious (having a living husband etc.) for a long lease of time.

394. Grand-father: The Grand-father of the
native of first half of the Uraga Nadiamsa will be
long-lived. If at all early death is denoted, it will
be only after the native’s 8th year.

Uraga Nadiamsa gives a long lease of life to the
native’s mother, and the first half to father.

397. Paternal grand-father: For one born in
the 2nd half of the said Nadiamsa, grandfather
will incur fatality in the native’s sixteenth year. In
case of the first half, the paternal grand- mother
will die early.
The 16th year of the native of first half of this
Nadiamsa will prove dangerous for his paternal
grand-father. If birth is in the 2nd half of this
Nadiamsa, it will be paternal grand-mother that
will be endangered during the native’s 16th year
of age.

398-399. Maternal relatives: The Moon
placed in Uraga Nadiamsa’s first half with
benefic’s aspect will cause destruction of maternal relatives for one born in Leo Navamsa
(Mitramsa is the navamsa owned by the Sun) in
Scorpio ascendant. If she be in second half of the
said Nadiamsa, there will be manifold growth of
members of maternal relatives’ families. They will
in addition be happy. The native will have three
maternal uncles while one of them will be somewhat fortunate.
rendering instead of friendly Navamsa. Here the
reference is not to Mars, but to Scorpio.
The Moon in first half of Uraga Nadiamsa,
while birth is in Leo Navamsa, Scorpio ascendant
(3 deg. 20 to 6 deg. 40°) will cause maternal
kinsmen’s destruction. Just by change of the
Moon into the 2nd half of this Nadiamsa, the
other condition being present, the maternal family will see growth.

former half of Uraga Nadiamsa will see evils for
maternal relatives. His father will incur debts
because of loss of wealth saved. As a result he will
be grief-ridden.

438-4441/.2. Durdhara Nadiamsa: If Jupiter
joins Saturn or is aspected by him, one born in an
immovable ascendant with Durdhara Nadiamsa
will incur destruction of elder brothers. He will
be endowed with younger coborns, will be wealthy and will be emaciated in childhood due to
grief. He will have three wives, be passionate,
given to anger but could be appeased instantly.
He will be endowed with learning and intelligence. He will be fond of sweet and sour juices,
will be lustful, dear to mother, sacrificing in nature and attached to women. He will be wealthy
on account of his fahter’s properties and will be
affectionate to his younger coborn. During his
childhood, loss of a paternal uncle will come to
pass. He will further be troubled by smallpox,
ulcers, dental and windy disorders. He will be
somewhat mixed in disposition with Satwa, Rajas
and Tamas. He will eat even uneatable food. He
will be skillful in gathering wealth. His first child
will be destroyed. He will attain surviving children
through second wife. Three of his daughters will
be longlived. These are the effects concerning
progeny and coborn.

Fora Beg मालिकायोगजातके। । ५०५। ।
atta क्लेशं विनिर्दिरेत्।
दरिद्ययोगे पापखेचरे! | ५०६। |
शुभद्ष्ठिविहीने च ata प्रपनुयद्ध्वम्।
505-506 1/2. Daridra Malika Yoga: If the
seven planets are disposed in the various seven
Rasis continuously counted from the Navamsa
Rasi of the 6th lord, the native will incur penury
and many difficulties in childhood. A malefic in
the 8th house without a benefic aspect will also
cause poverty.
Notes: Only seven planets from the Sun to
saturn are considered in Malika Yoga. They
should be progressively in continuous seven
houses. Thus 2 planets cannot be in one house.
When the Yoga starts from ascendant, it is called
Lagna Malika. From the 2nd Dhana Malika,
from the 3rd Vikrama Malika, so on and so forth.
To simplify, the Malika yoga goes under the name
of the house from where it commences.
The present work adopts a practice of
counting Malika Yoga from the Navamsa occupied by a certain planet. Here it takes the 6th
lord Moon. We should note Moon’s navamsa,
and from the Rasi identical with said Navamsa,
we should find seven planets in seven continuous
houses. Then the native instead of getting a Raja
yoga will suffer from poverty etc,
For Aquarius ascendant, a malefic in the 8th
house (Virgo) should be related to a benefic lest
he should cause penury.

disease.

with firm riches; his fame will specially increase
after the 25th year. He will enjoy pleasures after
63rd year and gather riches. He will incur abundant grief on account of loss of friends and
employer. In the 4th dasa, in a different kingdom

get his daughter and son married in that order. In

enmity with his friends. In the 2nd dasa, he will be
happy following birth of coborn when Saturn will
be in Libra. Saturn moving in Virgo will increase
prosperity of relatives. At the same time, the husband of father’s sister will also prosper and his
father will acquire happiness. Saturn in transit in

will cause auspicious effects. When he moves in
the end of Scorpio or in Sagittarius, he will cause
the death of paternal grand-mother. In trines
thereof he will cause the wellbeing of the inmates
of Chola kingdom(?) The ruler of his country will

550-5531/2. One born in Capricorn ascendant, Sachira Nadiamsa, having Saturn in
Aquarius with Mars will marry a girl hailing from
mother’s side. The 9th lord in Gemini Navamsa
while the 9th contains Rahu in aspect to the Moon
denotes a sister (i.e. the native’s coborn) being
interested in other men. Birthin Capricorn ascendant with Leo Navamsa while Saturn is in conjunction with Jupiter denotes an unfortunate and
wicked mother, who will not care to honour her
husband’s words. The 4th lord in Chaaravastha(?) in the company of Saturn will give a long
living mother, who will have secret relations with
others and be auspicious (?)
Notes: In these slokas four important events
are prescribed which are independent:
(1) Ascendant Capricorn, Sachira Nadiamsa, containing Saturn-Mars in it - bride from
mother’s side.
(2) The lord of 9th Mercury in Gemini
Navamsa, while Rahu is in the 9th house (Virgo)
in aspect to Moon - sister with illicit connections.
(3) Capricorn ascendant with Leo Navamsa (1.6. 23 deg. 20’ to 26 deg. 40’ of Capricorn
rising) while Jupiter and Saturn are together in
Rasi - unfortunate and wicked mother who does
not care for her husband, i.e. the native’s father.
(4) 4th lord Mars (in Charavastha?) with
Saturn - long-living mother but with illicit ties.

554-5591/2. Relatives: Saturn in Sachira
Nadiamsa with the lord of the 4th denotes an
abundantly prosperous maternal family. The
native’s father will have a (good) living. The 4th
lord in Sagittarius along with Jupiter, as Saturn is

sisters, Elsewhere it is stated that the ascendant
lord (Saturn) in the company of a planet that is in

coborns will correspond to the net Bindus in

common sign from 25 deg. 12’ to 25 deg. 24 of
space.
In Capricorn, this Nadiamsa rises from 10
deg. 12’ to 10 deg. 24. In such a case, the Moon

बन्धुवर्गे विचारः स्यात्करूरमासे |
594-5971/2. Evils: Saturn in the 2nd, Rahuin
the 9th, and the lord of the week-day of birth with
a malefic - this yoga kills the native’s wife in the
(very) second dasa, and immediately re- marriage
will occur to the native. Saturn coming 1n transit
to the Rasi represented by the natal Navamsa
position of the 2nd lord, or to a trine thereof, or
to the Rasi that is aspected by the 12th lord, will
cause danger to the native’s children. Maternal
uncles will be destroyed with Saturn’s transit in
the Rasiidentical with the natal Navamsa position
of the ascendant lord, or its trine, or the Rasi
identical with 8th lord’s Navamsa position or trine
thereof. In the absence of this, there will be severe
destrucion of some other maternal relatives. The
Sun’s transit in evil Rasis will in this connection
be revealing. The entire clan of relatives will be
grief-struck (on account of such death/deaths).
Notes: The lord of the week-day of birth is
brought into the picture in this 3-planet yoga.
Saturn in 2nd in Aquarius, Rahu in 9th in Virgo,
and lord of week-day of birth in association with
a malefic. This will cause wife’s death in the
native’s 2nd dasa, paving way for an immediate
remarriage. From this we understand that the
lord of the week-day of birth is not less important
than the lord of ascendant.
The seven week-days are named after the
seven planets from the Sun to Saturn. Ketu
denotes Tuesday and Rahu Saturday.
Exclude this 3-planet yoga in respect of
danger to children. The requirements then are:
Saturn should come to the Rasi in transit which is
identical with his own natal Navamsa position.
Alternatively he can transit the Rasi which at birth
is aspected by the lord of the 12th (i.e. Jupiter).
Such a transit of Saturn will endanger the native’s
offspring.
The Rasi aspected by 12th lord assumes
additional importance as the 12th house for
Capricorn ascendant is ruled by Jupiter, the significator of progeny.

सचिरांशकगे मन्दे पु दिवाकरे । । ६०३। |

603-6041/2. Father’s death: Saturn in
Sachira Nadiamsa while the Sun is in Mukunda
Nadiamsa will prove fatal to the native’s father or
a relative equal to father during third Dasa when
Jupiter in transit comes to the last degrees of
Scorpio, or when he moves in the sign Capricorn,
in adverse months. The mother will live for a long
time as a widow.
Notes: Mukunda Nadiamsa extends from
26 deg. 36’ to 26 deg. 48’ in movable signs; from 3
deg. 12’ to3 deg. 24 in immovable signs; and from
11 deg. 36’ to 11 deg. 48’ in common signs.
Mukunda is a synonym of Lord Vishnu who
is known so for He is the authority to bestow final
emancipation or Moksha. Thus this Nadiamsa
deals with one’s exit from this world. The Sun is
the indicator of father.
As per the present slokas, the Sun should be
in Mukunda Nadiamsa which denotes short life
to father.

610-611. Children: Saturn in Sachira
Nadiamsa will give a number of children. Many
children will be born dead. Ultimately the native

will be left with two or three surviving children.
The number of long-living children will depend
on the rays obtained, or on the Navamsa past, by
the Sth lord.
Notes: For Capricorn ascendant, Saturn in
Sachira Nadiamsa carries with him the blemish of
instant deaths of "many" children. In the end, two
or three will survive. However, the number of
surviving children can increase if the Sth lord
Venus obtains more Navamsas. That is, the Sth
lord Venus covering increased Navamsas in the
Rasi he is in at birth will have chance of giving
more surviving children. This clue is clear.
Another clue is to depend on the rays obtained by the 5th lord Venus. Rays are known as
Kiranas in Sanskrit. Calculating Kiranas is rather
an elaborate process all of which cannot be explained in the notes here. The reader is referred
to Saravali, English translation by R. Santhanam,

628-6321/2. For a Sachira Nadiamsa native,
Rahu in the 9th house will cause abundant difficulties and poverty in the 4th Dasa. There will

638-6421/2. Kunda Nadiamsa: For a native
of Pisces ascendant, if Mercury is placed in his
own sign in Kunda Nadiamsa, in the company of
the Sun and in aspect to 10th lord Jupiter, good
fortunes will accrue. Mercury becoming the lord
of the Moon sign and being in aspect to Jupiter,
will give paternal wealth to a Pisces native of the
Kunda Nadiamsa, in Vipra Kala. One born in
Kshatriya Kala will worship Lord Vishnu and will
serve an emperor. If the birth be in Vaisya Kala,
the subject will acquire livelihood by various business activities. He will gain wealth and grains, will
serve Brahmanas and be happy. One born in
Soodra Kala will serve a king, will own lands and
villages, will be an agriculturist and will have plenty of quadrupeds and corns.
Notes: From here through sloka 855, rules
from Budha Nadi are quoted.
To start with, Pisces ascendant, Kunda
Nadiamsa is dealt with,
Kunda Nadiamsa rules thus: From 26 deg.
24 to 26 deg. 36’ in movable signs; from 3 deg. 24”
to 3 deg. 30! in immovable signs; from 11 deg. 24
to 11 deg. 36’ of common signs.
In the first yoga, Mercury should be in
Gemini/Virgo in unity with the Sun and be
aspected by Jupiter. As a result the native will be
fortunate. The Sun is 6th lord.
In the second yoga, Vipra Kala should rise.
That is to say, birth ascendant should be 11 deg.
36’ to 11 deg. 39’ of Pisces. Further, the Moon,
being Sth lord, should be in the 4th/7th
whereupon Mercury begets Moon Rasi ownership. Jupiter should additionally aspect Mercury.
Result - paternal wealth.
The clues for births in other 3 parts of this
Nadiamsa are independent of second yoga mentioned above.
Kshatriya Kala of Kunda Nadiamsa in Pisces
extends from 11 deg. 39’ to 11 deg. 42’ of arc. One
born in this part will be a devotee of Lord Vishnu
and will be associated with an emperor.
Vaisya Kala of this Nadiamsa is from 11 deg.
42’ to 11 deg. 45’ when it rises in Pisces. The
native will be a trader true to the nathe of the part
of the Nadiamsa.
Soodra Kala is the last one fourth part, ie.
11 deg. 45’ to 11 deg. 48” in Pisces. Such a native
will be a rich person with agricultural lands, quadrupeds and the like.

643-6441/2. For birth in Pisces ascendant in
Vipra Kala of Kunda Nadiamsa, if Mercury is in
aspect to Jupiter, the younger coborns are
destroyed. The native will have older coborns, be
honourable and will have abundant paternal
wealth. He will have full brothers and stepbrothers as well.
Notes: Here Mercury’s position is not
prescribed unlike in the previous slokas. His connection in general with Jupiter is adverse for the
native’s younger coborn. But the birth should be
in the portion 11 deg. 36’ to 11 deg. 39” in Pisces.
However, the native will have full preborns,
paternal wealth and step- brothers. He will be an
honourable person as well.

645-6501/2. If the 7th lord is in aspect to
Jupiter, one born in Kunda Nadiamsa will worship Lord Vishnu and Lord Siva. He will honour
gods and Brahmins and will have (long-living)
parents. He will be happy, will gain fame in royal

the native’s middle age, his father will acquire
palanquins (1.6. some conveyance) and fortunes.
The native will indulge in self-praising and will

brother, his father will incur grief. Following this
the father will migrate to the southern part of the
country. He will serve an empcror and will

rules in general to Kunda Nadiamsa in Pisces
ascendant.
Mercury aspected by Jupiter will make the

effects given upto sloka 6501/2 are clear.

लग्नात् सप्तमगे चन्द्रे केसरीयोगजातके। । ६५५। |
पितृमातृचिरायुष्यं मातामहाश्चिरायुषम्।
655-6551/2. Kesari Yoga: One born with this
Yoga while the Moon is in the 7th house from the
ascendant will have long-living parents. His
maternal grand-mother will also be long-lived.
Notes: The author refers to Kesari yoga
where the Moon should be in Virgo in the 7th. We
should know the difference between Gaja Kesar
Yoga and merely Kesari Yoga.
Very few texts exactly describe Gaja Kesari.
Perhaps many compilers for want of brevity did
resort to brief mention of the conditions of Gaja
Kesari.
In Gaja Kesari the main constituent is
Jupiter. The Moon may or may not be in the
picture. I have given some relevant information

on this in Essentials of Predictive Hindu Astrology,
25 well as Brihat Parasara Hora, Part 1.
Coming to Kesari yoga, the constitutents are
two planets - the Moon and Jupiter by being
mutually angular.
Long life, renown, high intelligence,
destroying enemies and valour are the main effects of this Yoga. There is a view that one with
this yoga will highly prosper in the dasa ruled by
Venus.
Coming to our text, the Moon forming
Kesari Yoga should be in Virgo in the 7th. Jupiter
can be in any of the four common signs. As a
result, the native’s parents and maternal grandmother will enjoy long leases of life.

while ascendant lord Jupiter is in the end of
Gemini, or in Cancer. In the dasa of Sth lord, the
native in question will be very happy. His father
in this dasa will acquire royal honours, and be
happy after destroying his enemies. The native
will acquire palanquins, jewels etc. and will be

period will prove auspicious for his stepbrothers. He will enjoy great happiness through
his parents. There will be prosperity among his

fulfilled through his father. His maternal uncle
will obtain auspicious effets.
Notes: From slokas 660 to 6621/2, the effects
given are for one dasa. /Sutadhipadasakale’
means the dasa of the Sth lord and the rendering
is made as above accordingly. From sloka 663
onwards the words "Sukhadipadasa" appear
meaning the dasa of 4th lord. As the text so far
deals with Mercury from the beginning of this
portion of Kunda Nadiamsa, sloka 660 etc. should
be dealing with Mercury’s dasa and not Moon’s
dasa. Hence it should be Sukhadipa dasa kaale.
This terms continues in the following slokas also.

665-6671/2. Sun-Mercury conjunction: The
Pisces Lagna native (Mukunda Nadiamsa) having
the 4th lord in the company of 6th lord will see his
father in danger in his very first dasa. The father
will be troubled by fever with Sun’s adverse movements in evil signs. When Saturn is in Leo, the
native’s father will be in great distress. He will also
suffer from dysentery etc. The native’s afterborns being then born will pass away. His mother
will suffer from small-pox and the like. The family
will incur destruction of quadrupeds, grains and
the like.
Notes: With Saturn in transit in Leo, the
Pisces native with Mukunda Nadiamsa will have
destruction of younger coborns whose births
occur then. Saturn will aspect 3rd, Taurus. Also
see slokas 670-671 infra about loss of coborn
occurring in the bhukti of Venus, in the very first
dasa of the native.

acquire royal wrath. When Saturn transits in
Virgo Navamsa, troubles from king and minister
are to be expected. In the end of first dasa the
native will be greatly endangered.

period of Venus, in the first dasa of the native,
there will occur loss of coborn to him. After a spell
of mental distress and troubles to relatives, happiness will come to prevail.

677-681. Marriage: The native will be married after the 10th year of age or in the twelfth year
if the Moon in Kunda Nadiamsa is placed in
aspect to Jupiter. Elsewhere it is stated that for
Pisces Lagna native, if the Moon is in Cancer, he
will get married in his early childhood, or in his
second dasa. A benefic in the 10th house denotes
acquisition of wife from a highly prosperous family and the native will be fond of his virtuous wife
and be happy. He will obtain children in the 3rd
dasa and enter in royal service. In the very dasa
he will acquire wealth. He will be skillful in three
languages and be learned in literature, drama,
sastras etc. He will be a poet in Sanskrit and
Telugu. He will be honourable and quite famous.
His prosperity will be on increase, and after the
10th year of age he will be happy.

685-686. For a Kunda Nadiamsa native, the
Sun in Harina Nadiamsa will give Raja yoga effects according to Surya Nadi. Similarly, when the
Sun is in Ambuja Nadiamsa as Jupiter is is Harina
Nadiamsa. So declare the learned about these
three Nadiamsas.
Chandra Kala Nadi
Notes: Two different Raja Yogas are mentioned for a native of Kunda Nadiamsa native in
Pisces:
(1) The Sun in Harina Nadiamsa. This
Nadiamsa is entisted as Harini (meaning deer)
Nadiamsa in all the three sources consulted.
This Nadiamsa extends from 23 deg. 48’ to
24 deg. in a movable sign; from 6 deg. 0’ to 6 deg,
12’ of an immovable sign; and from 8 deg. 48’ to 9
deg. 0’ of a common sign.
The Sun in respective space of Harini
Nadiamsa in any Rasi for Pisces native with
Kunda Nadiamsa will give a Raja Yoga. The Sun
is the lord of the 6th, and being just in such a
position he can elevate the native to a worthy
position.
(2) In the second yoga the Sun and Jupiter
are required to be in certain spaces. The Sun in
Ambuja Nadiamsa while Jupiter is in Harini
Nadiamsa.
Harini Nadiamsa is explained above.
Pankaja (lotus) Nadiamsa extends from 29
deg. 36’ to 29 deg. 48’ of a movable sign; from 0
deg. 12’ to 0 deg. 24 of an immovable sign; and
from 14 deg. 36’ to 14 deg. 48’ in a common sign.
Jupiter should be anywhere in this manner.
These two yogas are from Surya Nadi.

words ‘Kumbhamsake’ and the rendering is ac-

690-691. Fourth dasa: Mercury in Kunda
Nadiamsa as the Sun is in Ambuja Nadiamsa will
in the 4th dasa cause following effects: In the
11th lord’s sub period, the native will enjoy
prosperity. And in the sub period of the 4th lord
(i.e. of Mercury), the subject will be happy, will
receive the mercy of Goddess Lakshmi (i.e. will
have increased wealth), be more prosperous and
will acquire many.sons.

692. Venus dasa: In the dasa of Venus
placed in Libra at birth, the native will enjoy
increased prosperity in Saturn’s (sub) period. He
will in Mercury’s sub period acquire fame and
mercy of the emperor.

701-707. For Kunda Nadiamsa: Mercury
and Jupiter being in own Rasis and Navamsas and
in aspect to each other denote a person who will
perform poor-feeding daily. He will be charitable
(do meritorious deeds) and his father will be
opulent. The dasa of Mars will give even results
(1.6. both good and bad in a balanced way) except
in malefic’s sub periods. Then the subject will
enter royal service. Influx of wealth is denoted in
the Dasa of Mercury. Happiness to father and
auspicious effects to half brothers will also come
to pass. However in the first dasa the subject will
undergo miseries, and his father agony. The first
half of Jupiter’s dasa will be comfortable, and in
the second half, interruptions will crop up. In the
same dasa and in the sub period of Mars, the
native’s younger brother will be troubled due to
adversities to his spouse and progeny. And in the
same Mars’ sub-period the native’s health will
decline. In the sub period of Mercury, in Jupiter’s
dasa, the native will experience great miseries.

714-716. Dhanada Nadiamsa: Ascendant
lord in Vargothama, the 2nd lord in Sura Nadiamsa and birth in Dhanada Nadiamsa denotes a
Brahmin. If birth be in the first half of Dhanda

He will be happy and endowed with an older
brother. His birth is in a small village. He will be
bold in disposition and dark in complexion.
Notes: From now on, discussions on Virgo
ascendant commence as per Budha Nadi.
Birth in Dhanada Nadiamsa in Virgo is
referred to. This Nadiamsa extends thus:
From 24 deg. 48’ to 25 deg of a movable sign;
from 5 deg. to 5 deg. 12’ of an immovable sign, and
from 9 deg. 48’ to 10 deg. of a common sign.
The first half of this Nadiamsa in Virgo is
from 9 deg. 48’ to 9 deg. 54 of arc.
One will be a Brahmin if Mercury is in Vargothama Navamsa, while Venus is in Sura
Nadiamsa. For Sura Nadiamsa, the various
zodiacal spaces are: from 1 deg. 24 to 1 deg. 36’
of a movable sign; from 28 deg. 24 to 28 deg. 36’
of an immovable sign; and from 16 deg. 24 to 16
deg. 36’ in common signs.
If the birth be in the second half of Dhanada
Nadiamsa, the Virgo native will be 2nd/3rd issue

730-7321/2. If the 9th house is occupied by
Saturn in the company of Mars, the native will be
bereft of after-born and be short-lived. If the
ascendant lord isin Libra Navamsa, the native will
have four brothers and sisters (in all). He will be
endowed with longevity. His older brother will
enjoy wide fame. Mars becoming the lord of the
9th Aroodha and being in the company of 10th
lord (i.e. Mercury) will cause Bhagya Yoga leading to prosperity. Further the native will perform
virtuous deeds.
Notes: Saturn and Mars in the 9th house will
affect adversely the life-span of a Virgo native.
If Mercury begets Libra Navamsa, one will
be long-lived, and will have four coborns.
The above two rules are general.
The 3rd rule, viz. Mars becoming lord of 9th
Aroodha Pada, and be in association with Mercury, the 10th lord, leading to prosperity is quite
suggestive. Mars, otherwise is an evil lord for
Virgo, ruling 3rd and 8th. But if he lords over
Bhagyaroodha (9th Aroodha) he becomes
capable of conferring riches, being in the company of 10th lord. In a way it implies association
of lords of 9 and 10.

Deva Keralam 7]
How to count Aroodha Pada is explained in
many standard texts. A brief narration is given
here for immediate use by the reader. Count
from a house the number of signs its lord is away.
Again count equal number of signs from the sign
occupied by that lord. The resultant sign is
Aroodha Pada for the first mentioned sign. For
example, for Taurus ascendant, Sth lord Sun is
placed in Capricorn. We get six signs from Leo
to Capricorn. Again from Capricorn when we
count six signs we reach Gemini. So Gemini is
Aroodha for the 5th house, in this case. And is
called Putra Aroodha.
There are certain exceptions. The reader
may consult Part 1 of Brihat Parasara Hora Sastra,
English translation, by R. Santhanam.

778-7781/2. Pisces Lagna - Kamala Nadiamsa: One born in this part of Zodiac while Saturn
occupies Aries Rasi and Leo Navamsa, will have
blood-red complexion. He will be learned. He
wili disregard his religion and join another
religion. He will incur grief on account of

Notes: From now on, the text deals with
birth in Pisces ascendant as told in Budha Nadi.
Kamala Nadiamsa obtains thus: From 5 deg.
48° to 6 deg. of a movable sign; from 24 deg. to 24
deg 12’ of an immovable sign; and from 20 deg.
48° to 21 deg. of a common sign.
Birth should be in the space between 20 deg.
48° 10 21 deg. of Pisces. Saturn should be in the
2nd in Aries from 13 deg. 20° to 16 deg. 40,
begetting Leo Navamsa (6th from Pisces). This
gives many troubles from enemies.
The native changing into another religion is
rarely discussed in the texts known to us. This is
one such useful yoga.

Deva Keraiam 75
180.7821/2. Saturn-Sun: If these two combine in Aries, the native will be righteous,
valorous, be a knower of all, given to anger, virtuous and be capable of breaking secrets. He will
be malignant (at times). The Kamala Nadiamsa
native having Saturn in a malefic Shashtiamsa will
be intelligent but inwardly cunning, He will be
libidinous and will have eyes resembling lotus-petals. He will be valorous, independent, addicted
to vices, given to instant anger, be pleased and
thin-bodied.
Notes: In the first place, exalted Sun and
debilitated Saturn joining together is discussed,
which is clear.
The second yoga dealing with Saturn in
malefic Shashtiamsa does not mention a specific
sign position to him. Nor the Sunis in the picture.
For details of Shashtiamsa - malefic and benefic -
please refer to Vol 1, Brihat Parasara Hora,
English translation by R. Santhanam.

left blank. Apparently it relates to Sukra Nadi.
Nirmala Nadiamsa extends thus: from 13
deg, 24’ to 13 deg. 36’ in a movable sign; from 16
deg. 24’ to 16 deg. 36’ in an immovable sign, and
from 28 deg. 24 to 28 deg. 36’ of a common sign.
Its first half in Libra extends 13 deg. 24’ to
13 deg. 30’. The first half covers both Vipra &
Kshatriya Kaalas.
The second half is from 13 deg, 30’ to 13 deg.
36’ in Libra.

874-877. The native will have two wives, be
prosperous but will do sinful acts. if Saturn is in
the Sth house, or Sagittarius or its trines, the
native’s grand-father will be troubled by
planetary evils, and by a weapon like dagger, will
indulge in thieving, and will swallow the wealth of
his kinsmen. Because of these evils, he will incur
grief on account of danger to his children. Worship and prayers offered to the Sun-God will
grant good health to the said relative, and one
(long) living son and happiness thereof. This applies to the native born in Nirmala Nadiamsa of
Libra ascendant, having Saturn in the 11th, falling
in exalted or own Navamsa, being in the company
of Venus. (That is Saturn and Venus in Leo, while
Saturn is in Libra, Capricorn or Aquarius
Navamsa.)

878-880. His grandfather will enter royal
service, be valorous, will have two wives, be lustful
and incur grief on account of his children. His
children will be destroyed due to his sins. However, after a long interval he will have birth of a
(long) living son and be happy. He will be rich
from birth. In the middle age, he will be grieved,
and will incur windy disorders, Due to his sins to
his kinsmen he will incur royal displeasure.

884-887. One born in Nirmalamsa will have
destruction of his paternal uncles. His father will
in his middle age acquire wisdom of agreat order.
In spite of having grains (i.e. eating comforts) and
wealth, he (the father) will be sickly. He will be
famous and happy. With the blemish of Mars in
the 9th house (birth in Nirmalamsa in Libra
Lagna), there will be troubles and fear in his home
(of birth). Right from the days of his being in-his
mother’s womb, his father would be troubled by
diseases. If birth be in the 2nd half of Nirmala
Nadiamsa, with Mars in the 9th house, his father
will have an untimely death, while additional
results are: evils to paternal uncle’s son and great
destruction of kinsmen. For birth in the first half
of Nirmala Nadiamsa (with Mars in the 9th
house) of Libra or Lagna, there will be illness to
father which will however be speedily cured.

888-891. Libra Lagna - Grand father:
Saturn, who is the lord of the house of paternal
grand-father, for Libra Lagna, joining Mercury
and occupying Libra Navamsa will give comforts
(health, happiness etc.) to the native’s grandfather. He will also prove auspicious to the
native’s elder brother. The native’s grand- father
will be famous and will have many brothers if
Rahu is in the 2nd house (in Scorpio) and the Sun
in the 10th house. Further his childhood would be
grief-ridden and marked by penury. He will be
emaciated. He will travel in the southern and
northern parts of the country. After marriage he
will become prosperous and celebrate birth of
children. He will be a wise man. These results are
applicable to the grand father of a person born in
Nirmala Nadi Amsa of Libra Ascendant.

good fortunes in the end of his youth and would

He will have two preceptors, be respectable, orthodox as prescribed by Sastras and be religiously
disposed. However for some part of his life he
would take to unorthodox means and then would
tread virtous path. These are when Saturn, the

Notes: In sloka 892, ‘Kuladwaye’ appears
which denotes that the native will adopt two
religions. In another version ‘Gurudwaye’ appears which indicates that the native will have two
preceptors.
Seeing the results of the native of being orthodox as prescribed by Sastras, I have adopted
‘Gurudwaye’ in translation.

inferior religion, for some.

first half of Nirmala Nadiamsa in Libra ascendant
will be pure (in conduct and character), will be

worthy progeny and be endowed with wealth,
grains etc.

grandfather of the native, there will be fortunes in
grandfather’s family. The native’s paternal grandfather will have much wealth. So also his full

898-902. Elder brother: For one born in the
first half of Nirmala Nadiamsa in Libra ascendant, with Mercury, Saturn and Venus in the 11th
house, elder brother will enjoy great prosperity

६. Chandra Kala Nadi
and will be skillful in two languages. His complexion will be blood-red and appearance auspicious (pleasing). He will have two wives and will
enjoy mundane pleasures. He will have many
sisters. The elder brother (of the Nirmalamsa
native) will have various business activities. He
will always honour his father’s words, will worship
God and protect (honour) Brahmins. He will be
happy with limited number of children and
limited wealth. He will live in three cities. He will
deal with gold and other articles imported from
different islands (i.e. foreign countries). When
the native will be in his Sampath Dasa (second
dasa) the elder brother will amass wealth, and will
in addition become widely famous. There will be
mercy of Goddess Lakshmi showering on the
family.

909-910. The number of coborns (for the
first half of Nirmala Nadiamsa, Libra Lagna) will
correspond to the dots contributed by Mars in his

Note: The word "Susmangali" is a specific
word in Sanskrit denoting a woman who 15 a
fortunate and devout housewife, with a long living
husband, the stress however being on husband’s
longevity.

birth should be. However this belongs to first half

911-914. For birth in first half of Nirmala
Nadiamsa, Libra Lagna, the lord of the 3rd, ite.
Jupiter, in Vargothama as the 7th house is bereft

= ` मातुर्मरणमादिशेत्।

917-919. The mother of the native of second
half Nirmala Nadiamsa of Libra Lagna will be
endowed with virtues and coborns. The native will

जननी मृतिमाप्नोति तुलांशे वृश्चिके शनौ।
शनेः तृत्तायपर्यये तुले मातूनाशनम्। । ९२० ।
920. Death of mother wil take place if
Saturn is in Scorpio occupying Libra Navamsa. In
the third round of his transit when Saturn reaches
Libra Navamsa, destruction (death) of mother
will take place.
Notes: The sloka is not very clear. The first
line seems to suggest early death of mother of a
Libra lagna native who has Saturn in Scorpio
Rasi and Libra Navamsa. The second line seems
to suggest that third round of Saturn, after crossing his natal position twice, will bring danger to
mother, when he reaches Libra Navamsa. Jn the
third round he can reach Libra Navamsa more
than once. Hence the meaning is not very clear. It
is left to the ingenuity of the reader.
