921-924. At the junction of Vipat Tara
(third) dasa mother will die when Saturn in the
first round is in Gemini. The native’s father will
die in the middle of the native’s Vipat Tara dasa.
Father’s death will be untimely (Apamrithyu).
Propitiations may prove helpful. If birth be in the
first half of Nirmala Nadiamsa, then the father
will be long-lived. He may live upto the 18/20th
year of age of the native. The native’s father will
fall sick immediately after the birth (of the native
in first half of Nirmala Nadiamsa) while evils to
kinsmen are also possible.
Notes: "Junction of Vipat Tara Dasa (3rd
dasa)" in this case may mean the period between
2nd and 3rd dasa, as mother is said to predecease
father. Also please note death of father is denoted
in the middle of Vipat Tara dasa. So there is no
question of mother passing away at the end of
Vipat Tara Dasa, i.e. junction of 3rd and 4th dasas.

६6 Chandra Kala Nadi
In sloka 922, the words "trines thereof
should relate to "Saturn in Gemini vide sloka 921".
In sloka 923, long life to father is predicted.
In the same breathsthg native is said to be 18/20
years of age at the time of father’s death. We do
not know as to what will be age of the father at the
time of the native’s birth. Hence these may not
mutually contradict.

925. Sakata Yoga: The Moon joining Rahu
in the 2nd house and being in Sakata Yoga (with
Jupiter) will bless the native with abundant
education, skill in consideration of the pros and
cons, and a high order of intelligence.

926. Should Saturn be in the company of
Venus and occupy own/exaltation Navamsa the
native will acquire education as prescribed for
his family of birth, and will be an examiner of
precious stones.

927-930. Jupiter in the 7th house for birth
in first half of Nirmala Nadiamsa, Libra Lagna:
Should Jupiter be in the 7th house (in Aries) for
native of first half of Nirmala Nadi Amsa in Libra
Lagna, he will be fond of mythological accounts
(Satkatha), and be learned in ancient Sastras and
Puranas. He will perform sacrifices, dig wells and
will do other acts of charity. He will be charitable
in general and will be of sacrificial nature. He will
attain fortunes through grandfather, be a great
donor and be happy. His education will commence in his fifth year. In the third year he will
have fear of fever (of some consequence). In his
childhood, he will be troubled by weak digestion
and will incur danger from chicken-pox. To ward
off these evils, the Moon god be propitiated and
donation of cobra’s image be made. Then sound
health and great happiness will prevail.

931-932. Nirmala Nadiamsa - Libra Lagna
- Lifespan: For one born in the first half of Nirmala Nadiamsa, long life will come to pass if
Jupiter in the 7th house (Aries) is in Vargothama
Navamsa. Infantile evils to lifespan, if any, will be
countered in respect of such a native just as
thunderbolt is resisted by the hill. One born in the
second half will face infantile evils to lifespan. If
however he survives (due to other combinations)
he will have a long life. One born in first half of
this Nadiamsa will have eyes resembling a lotus
petal.

933-937. Nirmala Nadiamsa - Other
results: (Birth in first half - continued) - He will
wed a woman who is gentle in nature. He will be
virtuous (or saintly). He will be fond of sweet and
sour juices. He will incur windy and phlegmatic
imbalances. He will also have bilious excesses. He
will have two names. He will be truthful in disposi-

Deva Keralam 87
tion. Being libertine, he will sometimes commit
adultery.
He will enjoy material pleasures and be dear
to king, if Venus joins Saturn. In addition, he will
sexually unite with prostitutes, will regret later
on and will win over his senses. He will be pleased
with his elders. His friends will be limited in
number. He will be thin and will have a long body.
He will be pure, highly intelligent and somewhat
timid. He will follow religious codes and be
charitable. He will dabble in Dharma Sastras.
Should Venus join (the ascendant having
Nirmala Nadiamsa, first half), the native will be
proficient and learned (in ancient Sastras) and
will be a true philosopher (knowing the nature of
Brahman). He will be endowed with pure conscience. He will be a skilful speaker, be somewhat
troubled by diseases, be given to anger, will have
a good residence and be rich.

938-9491/2. If the Moon is debilitated, occupying Kamala Nadiamsa and be in the company
of Rahu, the native will not be stable in disposition. Moreover, he will be somewhat a dunce. The
Moon conjunct Rahu brings to the said Libra
native fear from king and thieves. For one born in
Nirmala Nadiamsa of Libra, there will be grief on
account of coborn in middle age. If the Sun occupies the 10th house,the native will bathe in
Sethu (or sacred rivers), will attain abundant
meritorious fruits by bathing in the Ganges, and
will undertake a pilgrimage to Sethu (in the
South). In the middle of his life he will perform
good deeds and construct tanks and gardens.
During his pilgrimage he will donate a tawny cow
and will attain Punya (religious merits) and will
do cahritable acts on the banks of the Ganges. He
will help others to visit Ganges etc. and will be
happy. He will be endowed with a long life with
the Sun in the 10th house with Libra Navamsa.
Thus the results be understood to be materializing in the dasa of the 10th lord.
Lagna Lord’s dasa in its second half will
bring prosperity. In the dasa of Lagna lord, he will
attain a great deal of religious merits and wide
fame thereof. In Sampth Dasa (2nd dasa in sequence), coinciding with twelfth or twenty-third
year, in the month of Chaitra (one of the months
in Spring), he will get married to a girl coming
from the eastern part of his country. Ifthe 7th lord
isin Aries Navamsa, for a person born in Nirmala
Nadiamsa of Libra Lagna. The learned say that in
this context, the natal Moon of the spouse will be
in Aries or one of its trines. He will firmly support
his marriage (but) will have a remarriage after
some interval, in his twenty-fourth year, in the
dasa of the Lagna lord. There will be destruction
of wife in his 30th year and immediately thereafter
(?) remarriage will occur.

963. Second Lord’s Dasa - Nirmalamsa:
One born in this Nadaimsa’a first half will confront danger of (timely) death in the end of 2nd
lord’s Dasa. The probable months will coincide
with the Sun in transit in Scorpio, Sagittarius or
Capricorn.
964-9651/2. Indra yoga (continued): One
born in Nirmala Nadiamsa with this yoga will face
death in his 60th year. In his 54th year, his eldez
brother will pass away. In the native’s 27th year,
the said elder brother may contract fatality but
remedies will help. The native in his 9th year (one
sixth of 54) will incur many troubles and physical
ailments. After death he will attain good heavens.

966-967. The 12th lord joining Venus, and
Jupiter in the 11th along with the Sun denote that
the native will go to heavens and will have an

968. Raja yogas: There occurs a Raja yoga
for a native of one of the movable ascendants as
Venus occupies the sign Taurus, with Aquarius
Navamsa. As a result happiness (position etc.)
will come to pass after the age of 27.

969-9701/2. Venus aspecting or joining the
Aroodha of ascendant, 9th or 12th causes a Raja
yoga. Such a native will become a king or equal to
him, will be endowed with valour and be heroic.
He will be fond of drama, rhetorics (or ornamenting), poetry and music. He will speak fluently and
be truthful. He will be fond of scents, garlands and
robes.

Mars is in debilitation and the Moon is in the 3rd
house will cause an emaciated body, tuberculosis
and emission of blood in cough (Haemoptysis).

913-9771/2. Vishnu yoga: The Sun in Aries
with Leo Navamsa while Jupiter joins Ketu (elsewhere) will cause Vishnu yoga. Such a native will
prosper and will follow the codes prescribed for
a Vaishnative. Mars in ascendant while Saturn is
in the 12th house will also produce the said yoga.
The native’s childhood will be marked by grief.
There will be penury upto the age of 27. He will
have an emaciated body, be dark in complexion
and be endowed with an auspicious appearance.
He will realize the Supreme Spirit (theologian
etc.), will be wealthy, heroic, intelligent and fond
of promoting quarrels. He will be dear to king. He
will have two sisters and will also have younger
brothers. Venus causing Raja yoga, placed in the
11th house and occupying Aquarius Navamsa will
give younger and elder co-borns and make the
native libertine.
Notes: Vishnu Yoga described here applies
toa movable ascendant (Aries, Cancer, Libra and
Capricorn). In any case the Sun should be in
Aries, between 13 deg. 20’ and 16 deg. 40’ while
Jupiter is with Ketu elsewhere. Results are
prosperity etc.
Another kind of Vishnu Yoga for a movable
ascendant is formed by Mars being in ascendant
While Saturn isin 12th. The results of such Vishnu
Yoga are grief etc. as discussed in the text. Also
see sloka 980.

978-983. Avastha yoga: Upto the age of 27
the native will have a troubled life. Afterwards
there will be Rajayoga; particularly after the age
of 30 more favourable results will occur.
For one born with Kutila Nadiamsa of Libra
Lagna, with Vishnu yoga, there will be a troubled
(or unfortunate) sister while one will predecease
her husband. In the Kshema Dasa (4th Dasa) the
native’s will become widely famous. Vishnu yoga
is also caused by Bright half Moon placed in
Virgo. Such a native will be ever fond of listening
to accounts filled with jokes. He will be poor in
childhood and will incur grief. He will be skilful
in reading.others’ thoughts, will cheat his friends,
be skilful in his work, be wealthy, will cause
obstacles to the doings of his enemies, and will be
a tale-bearer. He will be a hindrance to others if
in the said Raja yoga, malefics are in the ascendant.
Notes: The sub-heading given in the text as
"Avastha Yoga" is not appropriate.
Hasyavastha of Moon is often referred.
This seems to be a confused term.

984-9851/2. In the Vishnu yoga caused by the
Moon as above, the nativ will enjoy a royal office
with seal (i.e. power), will indulge in many sinful
acts. He will follow the codes prescribed for a
Vaishnavite, will patronize his kinsmen and will
unite with his wife with exceeding passion. He will
cohabit (even) on days prohibited (by Sastras)
(i.e, on Amavasya and the two Ekadasi days).

986-9941/2, One born in Cancer ascendant
with Scorpio Navamsa will have a warm body. He
will heve phlegmatic imbalances and will be
somewhat troubled by diseases. Due to excessive
observance of (religious) vows and fasting, he will
be troubled by bilious disorders and cough. As a
remedy (to overcome these ills) he should worship an image of the zodiac, to be drawn on
husk-filled ground. The names of Nakshatras including Abhijit be written on the said space and
the Grahas (planet) be worshipped as prescribed
in the Vedas. The astrologer should be honoured
with gifts. Then the patient will get rid of the
disease. The said illness will occur in kshema
Dasa (4th Dasa) on account of dishonouring
Lord Siva (in the previous birth). The zodiacal
image (as above) be worshipped for two or three
weeks, with Prayers and donations. In the
presence of God (or in temples), worship of
Lakshmi and recitation of Mrityunjaya Japa be
performed. Nava Grahas be worshipped in one’s
residence as prescribed. Then comforts and
sound health will prevail. In the absence of these,
recitation of Mrityunjaya in a shrine of Lord Siva
be done and the image of Lord Siva (Siva Linga)
be installed to overcome the cough with emission
of blood (i.e. Haemoptysis). Some health and
prosperity can be acquired by donation of pots of
honey.
कन्याचन्द्रे राजयेोगे मकराशे प्रजायते। । ९९५। |

aga स्त्ीप्रजानां च पुप्रजस्वल्पमादिशेत्। | ९९६॥ |
995-996. Many daughters: The Rajayoga
caused bythe Moon placed in Virgo, in Capricorn
Navamsa will give results in the second half of
Kshema Dasa (4th Dasa). Such a native will have
many daughters and a few sons.

997-1002. Exessive Limbs & other effects:
One born in Vishnu yoga will have a virtuous
child, which is the 4th or Sth in order, when the
4th lord is in debilitation and the significator
(i.c.Jupiter) 15 aspected by Rahu, (for birth in
Kutila Nadiamsa of Libra Lagna). One of his
children may have excessive limbs, or be born
with teeth. The native’s elder brother will face
destruction, be sickly, will marry a barren lady
and will face obstacles in obtaining progeny. The
native’s younger co-born will get a virtuous wife
and will be prosperous. The native will be widely
famous on account of his brothers and will have
many children and much wealth. In his 16th year,
there will be danger to father coinciding with
Vipat Dasa (3rd Dasa), when Saturn is in the Rasi
denoted by the Navamsa occupied by Lagna
Lord, or its {1165 Destruction of his friends and
masters will also follow. Later on, in the subperiod of Mercury in the Kshema Dasa (4th
Dasa), ascendancy will dawn on the native.

Note: According to one view, the planet to
be aspected by Rahu is the lord of the Sth, vide
sloka 997 above.

1003-1009. Kutila Nadiamsa - Libra Lagna:
There will be Raja yoga effects in the eigth year
with Venus in the 11th house. When Saturn in
transit reaches Leo, or Gemini (i.e. Gemini/Leo),
the native will be troubled by a dangerous disease.
There will be great fear in the bhukti of the Sun.
To overcome these evils, gifting be resorted to. In
the (sub) periods of the Sun and Moon in Kshema
Dasa (4th Dasa), great danger will visit the native.
If he survives, he will be subjected to troubles like
destruction of prosperity. Death will result if
Rahu, playing a malefic role (as death-dealing
planet), reaches the Riksha Sandhi (in transit), In
the (sub) periods of the Sun and the Moon, when
Saturn retrogrades in the end of Leo, the native
will die on account of lung tuberculosis. The years
41, 38 or 36 of the native’s life may prove fatal.
32nd or 36th year may bring untimely end. If he
survives, the years after 41 will give Raja yoga
effects.
Notes: For Kutila Nadiamsa, see notes
under 8108 130-131 supra.
Riksha Sandhi is the junction of any of the
following pairs of Rasis in the Zodiac: CancerLeo; Scorpio-Sagittarius and Pisces- Aries.
नरकयोगः
Yoga for Attaining Hell
ik: योगे Ga —. Tae |
नरकं याति aes अधो गतै। । १०१० |
1010. Naraka Yoga: The first line does not
convey any clear meaning. It states: One born in
Vishnu yoga will for five nights perform Anushtana (religious ceremonies). Viththa is money
which makes, no sense. The second line means:
After death he will go to hell or will be fallen and
will attain mean worlds/birth.

1011-1012. Final Rites: For a native of Vishnu yoga caused by Saturn in the 12th, there will be
no performance of final rites by his son due to the
latter’s absence, residing in a different village, at
the time of the native’s death.
Notes: Saturn in the 12th implies Mars in
ascendant in case of Vishnu Yoga for a movable
accendant as per slokas 973-9771/2 supra.

1013-1015. Libra Ascendant - Indra yoga:
One born in Libra ascendant’s Indra Nadiamsa,
with Indra yoga caused by Venus- Saturn conjunction will be wealthy right from birth. He will be
highly intelligent, will have broad eyes, rich
friends and sons. He will have a long life, fame,

and be joyous and happy. He will be a great man,
rich with woman (or will have an excellent wife),
be endowed with wealth and grains, lustful,
learned, deceitful, honourable, fickle-minded
and intelligent.

yoga native is described by some authorities thus.
He will be somewhat sinful, be lustful, will after
some interval become knowledgeable, will
honour Gods and Brahmins, will be pleased with

1020-1025. One born in Indra yoga with the
Moon in Swathi Nakshatra (Indra Daivarksha)
and in Capricorn Navamsa will be dear to king,
libertine, eloquent in speech and highly intelligent. He will have an older co-born and many
friends. He will increase his fame through his
coborns. If the 9th house has a malefic, in the said
yoga, the native’s father will have professional
misunderstandings with his kinsmen. Evils to the
parents of the native be also predicted. Both these
would occur when the native was in his mother’s
womb, ic. before his birth. In the native’s first
year during Janma Dasa (first Dasa), agony to
father will occur. Good will occur to the native’s
paternal uncle and kinsmen. There will be happiness on account of birth of children and the like
in the house of paternal uncle. In the native’s
family the third son will do acts of bravery. The
native will be somewhat ill-disposed to his
kinsmen and will steal their wealth.
Notes: "Indra Daivarksha" denote Swathi
and Anuradha. These Nakshatras have Lord
Indra as Adhidevatha, ruling deity. Swathi 2nd
quarter falls in Capricorn Navamsa while
Anuradha has no such Navamsa. Hence in the
present context, Swati is relevant.

1026-1028. If the Indra yoga (i.e. VenusSaturn conjunction - ref. sloka 1013) occurs in the
9th house, the native’s parents will be long-lived.
So also will his elder brother be. One of the
native’s paternal uncles will have two marriages.
The native will be happy with abundant wealth,
prosperity and many sons. The Indra yoga
(referred to in sloka 1026, taking place in the 9th
house) denotes that the native’s father would in-

dulge in thieving in childhood. Due to blemish
acquired by the native’s mother, his father will be
somewhat grieved.

1029. The said yoga occurring (by the conjunction of Venus - Saturn) in the 7th Navamsa
(or in the 7th house, Aries) will confer a long
life-span on the native’s mother. His father will
take to righteous path.

1030-1031. The blemish caused by Mars in
the 9th house (for Libra Lagna) denotes that the
native’s father will indulge in foolish deeds, and
will in his middle age incur agony. He will also
suffer from complaints of weak digestion. To ward
off such blemish, remedies (as prescribed by
Sastras) and donation of an image of the Zodiac
be done.

1043-1046. Eye Disorders: In the sub
periods of the 6th lord in Janma Dasa, one will
incur eye-troubles which will (however) be immediately curable. This may coincide with the first
year of birth. The Sun joining Venus in Sth will
give eye troubles to the native in childhood in case
of Indra Nadiamsa. If the ascendant lord is in the
company of Rahu, ulcers on head or shank, or in
the heart, or facial diseases will occur, in
childhood. These can be countered by donation
of a cobra’s image. As a result, disorders of eyes
can also be avoided, acquiring healthy eyes.

1047-10491/2. Fortunate etc: One born with
Indra yoga occurring in the 9th house will be given
to enjoy pleasures. He belongs to a fortunate
family. If Venus is in Indra Nadiamsa, the native
of Libra will have two wives. So also his elder
brother. The native will have only one sister who
will incur difficulties in her middle age. If Venus
in Indra Nadiamsa joins Saturn, the native will
have two sisters, with a long and happy marital life
and daughters. One of the sisters will have an
adopted son.

1072-10731/2, Sun dasa - Ketu bhukti: The
native will keep the company of men born of mean
order. He will have various business activities. He
will suffer from digestive disorders and inflammatory fevers. He will visit foreign places (out of
distress). There will be grief in general, and fear
from kings and thieves. These effects are for
Ketu’s sub period in the major rule of the planet
Sun. There will be a mixture of good and bad
effects in Jupiter’s sub period (of the Sun’s major
period).

1074-10751/2. Sub-period of 6th lord: In the
2nd dasa, the sub- period ruled by the sixth lord
will cause bewilderment and physical troubles.
This applies to the Indra yoga native. These effects will come to pass when the Sun transits
malefic Rasis in the zodiac. The sub periods of
Ketu and Venus (in 2nd dasa) will also give effects
as above (i.e. like the 6th lord’s sub period). The
junctions of 2nd and 3rd dasas will cause troubles
according to learned men.

1076-1079, Great danger, Great -fortunes
etc. - In case of Indra yoga, note the dispositor of
the planet that is in aspect to the Moon. If the said
dispositor is in Leo Navamsa or Leo Rasi, the
effects are great fortunes and Raja Yoga (giving

position, wealth etc.). If Venus becomes a malefic
(by conjunction, aspect etc.), the Indra Yoga native in Rahu bhukti (of 2nd dasa) will face great
dangers. In Moon’s bhukti he will have fear of
dangerous fevers. To overcome the evils, the
Moon God should be worshipped and donation
of silver be made. When the 8th lord’s sub period
is in operation, malefic transiting the Ascendant
will cause difficulties to one’s kinsfolk.

1080-1082. Rahu in Vargothama: One born
in Indra Yoga with Rahu in the 2nd house in
Vargothamamsa, will be devoted to enjoyments.
He will suffer from illness during his childhood.
Fame and wealth will come to him in his middle
age. During his old age, he will be in the grip by
debts and will steal others’ wealth. In general he
will be proud of his lineage and will be famous
among his men, will be troubled by diseases, and
will be disposed charitably. He will have a notable
position with the king, and be highly honourable.
He will be famous and respectable among
physicians.

Vargothama position of Rahu will give auspicious

1084-10891/2. Effects for other dasa periods:
One born in Indra Nadiamsa with Indra Yoga, will
be happy in the dasa of Lagna lord. In the end of
the 8th lord’s sub period under the 9th lord’s
major period, danger and evils to mother be
forecast. So also in the sub period and sub-sub
period ruled by the Moon, in the dasa, second in
sequence. Danger from thieves and poison apart
from physical diseases will come to pass in the sub
period of Mars coinciding with Sun’s transits in
adverse, or weak Rasis. In the sub period of the
10th lord under the major rulership of the
eleventh lord, the native’s father will be initially
troubled by diseases and will be later on cured. In
the dasa and also sub period of the eleventh lord,
there will be obstructions to one’s income and
acquisition of a disposition marked by repugnance and hatred - all these due to blemishes
caused by Rahu. Troubles to coborn, and great
fear to self will strike in the sub period of the 11th
lord in eleventh lord’s dasa, or the initial phase of
it, will be somewhat distressful. In the dasa of the
10th lord, one born in Indra yoga will be ever
happy due to children (either birth of children, or
fame brought by children depending upon the
age in which this dasa occurs).

1092-10951/2. During the dasa of the second
lord, diseases will strike the native’s spouse and
progeny. The children of the Indra Yoga native
will have widespread business activities (in
general). In the beginning or end of the dasa of
the 2nd lord, or in the intervals of the planets
occupying the 2nd house, there will be fear of
death while great danger will strike in the dasa
that is the sixth in sequence. These apply to birth
in Indra Yoga and when Venus is placed in Indra
Nadiamsa. The native will die in the month of
Chaitra (with the Sun’s movement in Aries) and
will attain an excellent rebirth. These effects are
as per Yoga Nadi for one born in Indra Nadiamsa.
Such a native will attain prosperity after 20th year
of age. The length of his life will be 62 years.
। । शनिशुक्रयेगे शी तव्मंशेः। ।
Seethala Nadiamsa —
Saturn-Venus Conjunction
सुघाषष्ठयंशगे मन्दे चरराशिचतुष्ठये। । १०९६। |
1096-10971/2. Saturn - Yenus conjunction
for Seetala Nadiamsa: One born in Seethala
Nadiamsa will be endowed with a fair complexion
and auspicious (pleasing) appearance, if Saturn
is placed in the Shashtiamsa entitled Sudha and
be in a movable Rasi. One born in this Nadiamsa
(Seethala) in Pisces ascendant while Saturn is
with Venus in the 5th house will have auspicious
appearance but be dark in complexion.
Notes: From now on Seethala Nadiamsa in
Pisces ascendant is discussed. This Nadiamsa
rules thus: from 10 deg. 48’ to 11 deg. ina movable
sign; from 19 deg. to 19 deg 12’ of an immovable
sing and from 25 deg. 48’ to 26 deg. of a common
sign.
(There is also Seethala Shashtiamsa, and
Susitala Nadiamsa. These three terms should be
understood according to circumstances.)
According to the first condition, Saturn
should be in one of the movable signs in Sudha
Shashtiamsa. This particular Shashtiamsa extends from 18 deg. to 18 deg. 30’ of an odd sign,
and from 11 deg. 30’ to 12 deg. of an even sign.
(Sudha is a benefic Shashtiamsa. Sudha means
nectar, ambrosia, name of the Ganges.)
In addition to Saturn being so, Pisces should
1156 with Seethala Nadiamsa. As a result, the
native will be fair in complexion with charming
appearance.
In the second instance, Saturn-Venus should
be in Cancer to make the native dark-bodied, with
auspicious looks.

1098-10991/2. Jupiter - Saturn: One born in
Pisces ascendant while Jupiter is in Harina

Nadiamsa and Saturn is in Seethala Nadiamsa,
will be endowed with older coborns and be happy.
The native will be the 3rd issue of the family.
Children born after him will be destroyed (i.e. will
not survive). He will have (elder) brothers and
sisters. He will be honourable, valorous and
blessed with paternal happiness.
Notes: For Harina Nadiamsa, see notes
under sloka 685 etc.

1101-1104. Saturn - Ketu: In the above
mentioned position, if Saturn joins Ketu, during
the native’s childhood the father will be troubled
and will be quite emaciated. The native will not
have any after-born. His older brother will be
destroyed. The native will have his fortunes in a
foreign place. His father will have two sisters and
three wives, will be passionate, will have windy
imbalances (causing rheumatism, paralysis, artheritis and the like), will make his fortunes
through his own efforts, will have conveyances
carried by men (i.e. palanquins etc.), will incur
many difficulties due to sins committed against
Vaishnavites apart from incurring royal wrath.

1105-11071/2. Venus - Ketu: With these two
planets placed in Indra Nadiamsa in the company
of Saturn, while Saturn himself is in Seethala
Nadiamsa, the native’s father will hate Vaishnavites. As a result, he will incur difficulties, and
will lose "villages", lands and wealth. Mental
agony to fathcr will occur during the native’s
Janma (first) dasa as well as in the period prior to
native’s birth. Upto the native’s age of 3 yrs and 6
months, the father will face difficulties. Saturn in
eethala Nadiamsa (independent of Venus-Ketu)
will give difficulties to father till the native attains
fourth year of age. Later on, i.e. after the 4th year
till the end of Janma (first) dasa, the father will
enjoyprosperity.

48? to 22 deg. of a movable sign; from 8 deg, to 8

to 7 deg. of a common sign.

they are placed in one Nadiamsa (12’ of arc)
entitled Indra, as above. Additionally Saturn
should be in Seethala Nadiamsa.

अलक्ष्मीनाशनात्सद्यः पिता लक्ष्मीकरक्षवान्।
1108-11161/2. Saturn in 5th: Saturn placed
in the 5th house in Seethala Nadiamsa will cause
distress to father, who will suffer from affections
of gums, fever, dysentery and be fear-struck.
During the native’s Janma (first) dasa, the native’s
father would be subjected to a great deal of mental agony and perplexion. The native’s coborn will
die as they are born. He will sufffer from smallpox
and incur danger. The native’s father will be
troubled by (dangerous) fever if the Moon is in
blemished Nakshatras. Necessary propitiations
be done to ward off this evil. This is when Saturn
is in the 5th in Seethala Nadiamsa. By donation of
gingilee and an image of Lord Vishnu, the native’s
father will regain health. He will further earn
royal favours and will have destroyed enemies.
Some texts prescribe donation of clarified butter
(distinct from ghee or Ghrita) (in place of gingilee), image of Lord Vishnu, and gold, following
which the prosperity of father will increase, and
he will regain sound health. By giving in donation
to a Daivagna (astrologer) four vessels made of
lead, filled with ghee, and just a Nishka of gold (a
golden coin of ancient evaluation), father’s health
will become normal, his poverty will instantly
vanish and he will be blessed by Goddess
Lakshmi.

1117- 11201/2. Venus in the 5th: If Venus is
in the 5th house along with two malefics, gold and
clarified butter be given away in charity when
Goddess Lakshmi will bless the native’s family.
There will be prosperity to the native’s father
after the native’s 5th year (i.e. from 6th year onwards) and ever-lasting happiness. In the 4th year
there will be some happiness while the Sth year
will give mixed results, both auspicious and inauspicious, to the native’s father. Due to sins
committed against Vaishnavites, the father will be
in difficulties on account of kings and ministers in
his birth place, during the time of the native’s
being in Janma (first) dasa. More specifically in
the middle of such dasa. During the native’s third
year of age, there will be fear of untimely death to
the father; later on till the end of the dasa in
question, happiness, and destruction of enemies
will accrue to father.

1124-11301/2. More about father and other
relatives: Saturn in the Sth house joining Venus
will give prosperity to father in the beginning and
concluding parts of the native’s first dasa. In the
birth country, prosperity to father will be limited,
while it will be abundant in foreign places. Saturn
in movement in Virgo during the native’s Janma
(first) dasa will give wealth (to father). While
Saturn reaches Virgo as Jupiter is moving in transit in Gemini, the native will have sacred thread
ceremony (Upanayana due to a Brahmin, called
Dwija, which gives him second birth). Marriage
to elder brother will occur during the native’s
Janma (first) dasa when Saturn is in Virgo, while
Jupiter moves in the end of Aries, or in Taurus.
During Janma dasa if Jupiter transits the sign
Aries, the native’s father will gain happiness. His
half brothers will acquire progeny. If Jupiter is
found moving in Pisces, the native will acquire
younger coborns. If Jupiter transits the end of
Pisces, or be in Aries, there will be evils to the
native’s elder sister. Saturn in Seethala Nadiamsa
will cause loss of coborn at a later date. The
husband of the native’s half sisiter will enter in
royal service.

1133.11351/2. Saturn in Seethala or
Mahamaari Nadiamsa: Saturn placed in
Seethala Nadiamsa or in Mahamaari Nadiamsa
[instead of Sthithau, the word Sanau appears in
another version] will give the native two sisters,
who will be long lived, and also two brothers. The
native will be worshippable. His step brothers will
(also) be long-lived. The native’s sister will obtain
dead children or be barren, but be happy (i.e. will
get progeny) after a long interval. In the native’s
second dasa, the husband of the native’s elder
sister will become wealthy. During the third part
of Sampath (2nd) dasa (1.6. in the last one third
part of the dasa in question), the brother-in-law
of the native will obtain happiness.
Notes: Mahamaari Nadiamsa is also known
as Mahamaaya Nadiamsa. Mahamaari in the
Southern part of India ts noted as deity incharge
of small-pox, chicken-pox, cholera etc. Mahadhoothi is another name for this part.
Mahamaari Nadiamsa extends from 15 deg.
to 15 deg, 12’ of arc in a movable sign; from 14
deg. 48’ to 15 dég. in an immovable sign; and from
0 deg. 0’ to 0 deg. 12’ of a common sign.

1136-11401/2. Saturn - Mars: If Saturn is in
the 7th house while Mars is in Mahaamaari
Nadiamsa, following effects will occur. During his
childhood, the native will be plagued by cholera
and will incur destruction of coborn. His maternal
grand- father will be long-lived. The native will
have two maternal uncles. One of them will be
destroyed (i.e. will die early) while one will be
long-lived. Evils to maternal uncle will come to
pass when Saturn in transit reaches the Rasiidentical with the Navamsa position of ascendant lord,
or its trine. This will also bring grief to maternal
grand mother. In the 2nd dasa, for birth in
Seethalamsa when Saturn comes to the end of
Libra, or when he moves in Sagittarius, evils to
matenal grandfather will occur. During Saturn’s
transit of Virgo, the first dasa of the native will see
birth of children to maternal uncle. And also the
native’s step-sister will acquire progeny.
Notes: Cholera indicated in this part of the
text can also additionally mean small-pox, chicken pox etc. true to the name of the Nadiamsa Mars
is expected to be in.

1141-11421/2. Janma Dasa: During the
native’s Janma (first) dasa, with Jupiter moving in
Aries, and Rahu in Libra, prosperity will dawn on
his father. This will also bring auspicious effects
to father in royal assembly. In the first round of
Saturn, in the native’s Janma dasa, great happiness to father will occur, apart from Raja Yoga
pronounced when Saturn journeys in Libra.

1143-11461/2. Other Effects: When Saturn
reaches the Rasi identical with the Navamsa occupied by the 6th lord at birth, or its trine, the
native’s father will incur abundant grief on account of his friends. Father’s enemies will be
destroyed during the dasa of the native’s 6th lord,
or when Saturn reaches the Rasi identical with the
Navamsa occupied by 6th lord or its trine. There
will be some troubles to the native’s elder brother
(i.e. illness and the like) while the native’s kinsfolk
will pose some nuisance. In the end of the first
dasa, patenal relatives will be under a spell of
mental agitation. Lack of rains and occurrance of
drought will tear his country(?) Due to change of
king, the native’s father will immediately become
prosperous.

1147-11501/2. Dasa Effects: One born in
Seethala Nadiamsa’ will gain wealth in the dasa
that is the second in sequence. When Saturn in
transit reaches the verge of Libra, or be in Scorpio, the Seethalamsa native will get married in

Chaitra (the Sun in Aries), in the bright fortnight
of the moon. In the 3rd dasa, during its last onethird part, when Saturn in transit reaches the end
of aquarius or be in Pisces, evil to father will come
to pass. During the Seethalamsa native’s 23rd
year of age, the father will take to ascetic order
(following some calamity in the family). In the sub
period of a malefic, in the dasa of the 9th lord, the
native’s father will die. When Jupiter in transit
reaches the Rasi identical with the Navamsa occupied by the Sun or its trine, father’s health will
suffer, while there will occur mental agony to
father in the months indicated by the Sun’s adverse transits.

1151-1155, Evil Rasis: According to the
learned in astrology, when Saturn is in Seethala
Nadiamsa, the evil Rasis (Kroora Rasis) and evil
months (Kroora Masas - based on the Sun’s transit) are: Cancer, Leo, Scorpio, Pisces, Aquarius
and Cancer. When Rahu, Ketu or Saturn come to
occupy such a Rasi, the native’s kinsfolk will be in
trouble. Grief to father, and mental agony to
mother etc. will come to pass when Saturn moves
in one of the above Rasis. Thus be understood the
effects of sub peiods of the malefic planets m the
major rulership of the dasa that is 2nd in sequence.

1156-1160. Other Effects: The said malefic
bhuktis will in addition cause physical ailments to
father and such other relatives and evils to one’s
kinsfolk. These are due to birth in Seethala
Nadiamsa with Saturn in the 5th house. In the sub
period of benefic planets (in the 2nd dasa), happiness to father, and prosperity will come to pass.
In the 4th dasa, there will be specially effects of
Raja Yoga (to the native). After 24th year of age,
he will attain prosperity and widespread fame.
One born in Seethala Shashtiamsa will have
Rajayoga effects if Jupiter (at birth) is in the 9th
house, or when in transit he comes to an
angle/trine. Acquisition of conveyances (like
palanquins etc) will also come to pass. After the
27th year, prosperity, acquisition of quadrupeds
and wealth will also take place. The native will
become widely famous through royal service, and
will gain supreme positions.

1161-11621/2. Rahu dasa: The sub periods
of Saturn and Ketu, in the Dasa of Rahu, will
cause physical ailments, while in the sub period
of the Sun, there will be fear of untimely death,
and great danger. Cough and breathing disorders
will also result in this sub period. To overcome
these blemishes, (suitable) propitiations be done.

1163-1166. 4th Dasa & Rahu Dasa: In the
sub period of Saturn, in Kshema (4th) dasa, there
will be destruction to friends and employers.
There will also be some fear out of fevers, but
happiness due to royal patronage will also occur.
For one born in Seethala Nadiamsa, with Moon
placed in Virgo, the middle portion of Rahu dasa
will give effects of Raja Yoga. He will gain aname
due to construction of colonies [in charity, not as
a professional builder] and will be intent upon
serving the king. During Rahu dasa, he will suffer
from diseases due to sins of causing evils to his
friends. Later on he will regain health. In the end
of Rahu dasa, he will incur some difficulties and
(yet) be happy. (That is, end of Rahu dasa will give
mixed results.)

1189-1191. Should the 12th lord be in his
exaltation Navamsa while Jupiter placed in Sagittarius is in Cancer Navamsa, the subject will attain
heavens after death. One born in Seethala
Nadiamsa as Satura is conjunct Venus and Jupiter
in Cancer Navamsa, the native’s father will attain
Brahma Loka (i.e. Sathya Loka, the highest of the
worlds of merit to be attained after death). He will
enjoy Raja yoga effects, will have palanquins and
the like, will then go to heavens and will be reborn
as an excellent soul.

1207-1211. Sagittarius Lagna, birth in
Dhaatha Nadiamsa, Saturn placed in Scorpio
(upto sloka No. 1218): One born in Sagittarius
Lagna’s Dhaatha Nadiamsa, having Saturn in

be destruction -of his elder brother and his

province in service of a mean ruler. He will have
two wives, will have progeny and will have Amalaa
Yoga (i.e. a benefic in the 10th house, in Virgo).
He will attain palanquins due to his livelihood in
fields of Vedic lore. In the 4th dasa, he will get a
great deal of happiness, but in the middle portion,
there will be (heavy) expenditure. Happiness will
accrue to him in the 2nd half (of Kshema, or 4th
dasa). He will gain villages.

पूर्वभागे प्रजातस्सः आदि खण्डे तु जातकः।
oe ge By दयमाप्नोति मध्यखण्डे चिरायुषम् | । १२५४ I
शि पूर्वखण्डे पूर्वभागे तु |
मध्यखण्डे चिरायुष्यं गुहाशे चैव जायते। | १२५५। |
1254-1255. One born {3 the first one-third
part of first half of Guha Nadiamsa will have a
short life, and the one in second one- third (middle of the 3 parts) will be long-lived. And the one
born in the first one-third of second half of this
Nadiamsa will have a long life.
Notes. These two slokas need some
reconstruction for proper understanding.
Vasishta and Garga seem to have made each
half of Nadiamsa as 3 sub divisions, 1.6. one
Nadiamsa has 6 parts. (This is only my assumption
as otherwise there is no authentic teaching about
a Nadiamsa being made into six sub divisions). In
which case the above rendering conveys something. Yet the words "Poorva Bhaga" in the first
line of Sloka 1255 are out of place.
Alternatively avoiding a debate whether a
Nadiamsa has six parts, we can simply form two
specific clues from-the above two slokas:
1. Guha Nadiamsa - first half: the native will
be short-lived.
2. Guha Nadi Amsa - Second half: the native will be long-lived.

The second half of this Nadiamsa seems to
be better for life span, devoid of Balarishta, as
could bé seen in S. 1246 - 1247 above (Yavanas).
Similarly first half of this Nadiamsa gives poverty
and second half of riches (sloka 1261 infra).

1333-1335. Balarishta: One born in an immovable ascendant with Saturn in the 6th and the
Moor in the Znd will incur infantile mortality. If

an end) death will be certain in the 3rd/6th year.

effects of infantile mortality. Alternatively evils
will strike the native’s parents and coborn.

will (thus) be evident, according to sages. His
older coborn will have progeny.
Note: The expression "Soothika Graha"
should be "Soothika Griha" in §.1344 and

chamber here and accordingly the translation is
made. Anyway, these lines repeat twice while
only once it is meaningful.

1339-1365. Dasa effects: In the sub period
of an afflicted planet during the main period of
the 8th lord, the native’s kinsmen will face danger
and the native will himself be diseased in a foreign
place. He will gain wealth in the concluding part
of the 2nd dasa and will have professional gains.
His third dasa will generally inflict troubles to his
wife and children, while the sub period of Saturn
thereof will give beneficial results. In the sub
period of Rahu in 3rd dasa, travels and financial
improvements will come to pass. Malefic periods
will cause troubles to coborn. In the 24th year the
native will face difficulties in dealing with others.
The middle one-third portion of Vipath (3rd)
dasa will cause difficulties due ot indigestion (i.e.
stomachial disorders). He will ever be blessed
with the mercy of Goddess Lakshmi (in general).
He will be the fifth issue, or the third or the fourth
(i.e. he will not be among the first two). When the
4th lord Mars is with Saturn, birth and dwelling
of the Kanthaa Nadiamsa native will be in northsouth spread street in the region of Maharnava.

1372-1376. Parents: If Mercury is in the
3rd/6th/8th house while Saturn is in the company
of or in aspect to the Sun, the native’s mother will
pass away in childhood, and a step-mother will
step in his life. His father will be long-lived and
will have secret sexual urges. There will be fifteen
or sixteen coborns to him (father). Eight of them
will be sisters two of whom will live in respectable
abodes. His paternal uncle will also be long-lived,
be endowed with progeny. One of them will have
many children, even out of whom female children
will exceed male children. The native’s first paternal aunt will become a widow, but be long- lived.
Because of step-mother, the said aunt will worry
about children. Due to her own good fortunes, a
female child will be born to her after a long
interval.

1379-1380. Mudgara Nadiamsa - Sagittarius ascendant: Onc born in the second half of
Mudgara Nadiamsa in Sagittarius ascendant will
be in poverty right from birth and will be a
Purohit of the town (family priest performing
ceremonies prescribed by scriptures). He will be’
restless to have progeny as he will beget children
who will only pass away as they are born. Danger
will occur to his spouse in his fourth Dasa. He will
in the same Dasa live in foreign lands.
Notes: Mudgara Nadiamsa appears in some
places as Mudgala.
This Nadiamsa extends from 4 deg. 36’ to 4
deg. 48’ of a movable sign; from 25 deg. 12’ to 25
deg. 24 of an immovable sign and from 19 deg.
36’ to 19 deg. 48’ of a common sign.
These two slokas give effects for birth in
Mudgara Nadiamsa in Sagittarius (19 deg. 36’ to
19 deg. 48’) without reference to any planet as
such. Tersely, the native will never be happy in
terms of financial acquisition, will have children
with short life- spans and his wife will pass away
early.
Since a great deal of prosperity is indicated
for birth in 2nd half of this Nadiamsa vide slokas
1384-1386, we may conclude that the above effects are for birth in first half of the said Mudgara
Nadiamsa, 1.6. 19 deg. 36’ to 19 deg. 42’ in Sagittarius.

in Sagittarius, vide slokas 1379-1380, it is stated

whereas the current sloka, 1398, indicates the
subject being wealthy through paternal sources.
Obviously these effects will come to pass only
when the Sun is in the 10th house in aspect to
Saturn. Thus the condition laid down in sloka
1397 applies upto sloka 1404 infra.

11 कटक लग्नम् (चम्पकांश) । ।
Cancer Ascendant — Champaka Nadiamsa
चम्भकशि पूर्वभागे कुव्टरि तुं प्रजायते।
विप्रजन्मा सुखी नित्यं सीमन्ततनय स्मृतः।॥ १४१२॥
महानदीप्रान्त्देशे शिवालयसमीपगे।
दकषणेततसवीधोष प्रागे जननं स्मृतम्।
1412-14131/2. Cancer ascendant - Champaka Nadiamsa: The native of first half of Champaka Nadiamsa, in Cancer ascendant, will be
aBrahmin, be ever happy and will be the first issue
of the family. He is born near a temple of Lord
Siva, in the Mahanadi region, in a big city, in his
relatives’ house. The street of birth is spread from
north to the south. The house is located in the
eastern corner.
Notes: Champaka Nadiamsa is spelt out as
Chambaka Nadiamsa (in Sanskrit text also).
Champaka Nadiamsa rules from 5 deg. to 5
deg 12’ of a movable sign; from 24 deg. 48’ to 25
deg. of an immovable sign; and from 20 deg. to 20
deg. 12’ of a common sign.
The first half of this Nadiamsa in Cancer
extgends from 5 deg. to 5 deg. 6’ of space.

red in complexion and will have an auspicious

Notes: In the 2nd line of sloka 1414, the
following expression is found which is not added
to the English rendering to avoid confusion.
"Lagnaanthye Chaapaseethage" which does
not clearly convey the condition laid down to give
the said effects. Lagnaanthye means end of
(Cancer) ascendant. Chaapaseethagau means
the Moon in Sagittarius. Dhanurasige replaces
the latter expression in some texts, which means
(some planet) in Sagittarius.
Apparently, the description of the native as
above shall exist if the Moon is in Sagittarius while
birth is in Cancer Lagna’s Champaka Nadiamsa,
in first half.

1 ए Hed जातो सचिवो वा चमूपतिः।
तिष्कमीश्वरभाषितम्। | १४३०।
अष्टत्रिंशेअथवा चेति पित्रार्जितघनाढ यवान्
1430-14301/2. Kahala Yoga: One born in
Kahala Yoga will be a minister or an army chief.
He will own more than ten thousand gold coins.
He will speak like a lord (i.e. superior in speech).
His 38th year will give him paternal riches.
Notes: It is not understandable whether
Kahala Yoga is discussed as a general principle or
with reference to Champaka Nadiamsa in Cancer
alone.
Kahala Yoga is formed when the 4th lord
and Jupiter are in mutual angles. Alternatively,
the 4th lord should be in his own sign or in exaltation. He must as well join the lord of the 10th.
For Cancer ascendant, Venus is the lord of
the 4th. He should be in an angle to Mars. Or
Venus should be in Libra/Taurus/Pisces in association with Mars. According to the text,
Kahala Yoga gives wealth exceeding "10,000 gold
coins" while the native’s 38th year will bestow on
him paternal wealth as well.
With Kahala Yoga in general according to
Parasara, the native will be endowed with a complete army of chariots, elephants, horses and 17fantry. He willlord over villages, be energetic and
adventurous.
As per C.G. Rajan, the 3rd kind of Kahala
Yoga is torn thus : The lords of fourth and 9th
should be in mutual angles while the ascendant
lord is endowed with strength.
Foolishness is also a part of Kahala Yoga.
Mantreswara gives a fourth kind of Kahala
Yoga in his Phala Deepika. The dispositor of the
dispositor of ascendant lord should be in exaltation, or own sign, identical with a trine/angle
counted from the ascendant.
Also see notes under sloka 1813 infra.

1431-1433. Dead children and remedies:
One born in the former half of Champaka
Nadiamsa of Cancer ascendant will obtain many
children dead. To surmount such possibilities,
Mantras and Japas for Lord Siva be performed
for 360 (days) whereafter a ten thousand such
blemishes will vanish and the native will beget
(long-living) progeny.

1446-1450. The Sun-Mars: These two
planets conjunct in the 10th or the 4th will give
following results to the native of former half of
Champaka Nadiamsa in Cancer ascendant. He
will be dark in complexion, will enjoy pleasures
will be courageous. He will have a concubine and
be fond of drama, poetry etc. and will be interested in joining a relative’s wife. He will in addition be bountiful, truthful and be fond of scents,
garlands and robes. His prosperity will be after 12
years while he will be wealthy after 16. In his 25th
year he will obtain Raja yoga effects. He will join
whores as well as his sister-in-law. He will be
interested in reciting Mantras that please Lord
iva.

The native of Champaka Nadiamsa’s first half,
with the Sun placed in Kanta Nadiamsa will be
amused during sexual union, will be eloquent in
his speeches in royal assembly, endowed with skill
and contrivance, will be able to do even impossible things and will own hundred thousand gold
coins due to Lord Siva’s mercy.

period of the 9th lord during the Dasa of Rahu,

He will have (futile) movements and will earn
royal displeasures.

1480. Rahu bhukti: The second half of

1481. Venus Sub-period: The sub-period of
Venus (in the dasa of Rahu, in case of birth in
Cancer ascendant, Champaka Nadiamsa) will
cause to the native a great deal of fortunes. However, the native’s youngest co-born (last of the
family) will develop evil thoughts. The native’s
friends and relatives will be destroyed. If Venus is
in "Sathamsa", auspicious effects will follow.
Notes: In this sloka, Rahu dasa, Venus subperiod, for a Cancer native is discussed. This
sub-period is said to give auspicious effects about
native’s fortunes, but adverse effects concerning
friends and relatives. The last line of the sloka
suggests good results in this respect also,
provided Venus is placed in a certain position.
‘Sathamsa’ is not a meaningful astrological term.
At best it could mean 100th degree or 100th part,
which makes no sense. Maybe it should read
‘Ucchamsa’ - exaltation Navamsa. However, obtaining a new meaning using Katapayadi numerical is not advisable at this point.

यौवने दारशोकादिस्त्रयोविंशतिवत्सरे।

1497-1499, Kalakoota Nadiamsa: One born
in Kalakoota Nadiamsa, and having the Sun in the
7th Navamsa, will have injuries on privities and
other parts. He will lose his (first) wife in 23rd
year in his (very) youth, and will marry for the
second time in the beginning/end of the 24th year.
After marriage he will follow the path of Wisdom.
Jupiter in Brahmagnanamsa (?) being in an
angle/trine from ascendant Sagittarius will make
one a great yogi meditating on the Supreme Being
who will take to ascetic order due to a family
calamity.
Notes: This Nadiamsa, Kalakoota, should
rise in Sagittarius itself. The Sun should be in 7th
place in Navamsa chart.
Kalakoota Navamsa extends from 0 deg. 36’
to 0 deg. 48’ of a movable sign; from 29 deg. 12’ to
29 deg. 24 of animmovable sign; and from 15 deg.
36’ to 15 deg. 48’ of a common sign.
Jupiter is required to be in the Nadiamsa
entitled in Brahmagnana. We have no such
Nadiamsa enlisted. We have ‘Braahmi’ which
means the personified energy of Brahma
regarded as one of the eight mothers of created
beings, said to attend Skanda. Seeing similarity
in names, the text apparently uses a nearsynonym.
Braahmi Nadiamsa extends from 0 deg. 24
to 0 deg. 36’ of a movable sign; from 29 deg. 24 to
29 deg. 36’ of animmovable sign; and from 15 deg.
24 to 15 deg 36’ of a common sign.
Jupiter should be in trine from Sagittarius

1561-66. Long-living Mother etc.: Should
the 4th lord Moon join Ketu, in aspect to Jupiter
and at the same time beget Gemini Navamsa, the
native’s mother will be endowed will longevity.
Further, he will be honourable while his older
sister will be long-lived and will be auspicious
(will have a long-living husband). One of the
native’s brothers will also have a long life, while
other brothers will destroyed (early) in their
respective Maraka periods. The native will be
troubled by small- pox in the sub-period of
Jupiter in the second dasa. He will also suffer
from indigestion, fever etc. and will incur
Balarishta effects to some extent. During the said
dasa, when transit Saturn is found in Gemini (or)
in the 11th year, there will be some troubles.
Twelfth year will cause loss of father. The 6th lord
Mercury, denoting maternal kinsfolk, being in his
exaltation Rasi with Aries navamsa, should at
birth be aspected by Saturn. In that event, Saturn
in transit in Gemini will cause danger to maternal
kinsmen in the 3rd dasa of the native.

1567-1568. New house: If the Moon, ruling
the 4th house, is in Gemini Navamsa, one will
obtain a new house in Rahu dasa, Venus bhukti,
provided the said Moon is in aspect to Jupiter.
Experiencing some expenses initially, the native
will gain financially and obtain the mercy of Goddess Lakshmi for his family. He will abundantly
gain wealth, but his spending will be in equal
measure.

1584-1588. NB: These five slokas, seemingly
narrating Sundara Nadiamsa of Capricorn ascendant suffer incoherence and lack of meaningful
sequence. Partly these relate to the king of the
country and partly to a native. The following
slokas from 1589 onwards give suitable account
of the same Nadiamsa. Hence the present five
slokas (nine lines in all) are to be ignored
Sundara Nadiamsa is not enlisted which
deals with Capricorn ascendant in this part of the
text. I have however a feeling that Surasundari
was wrongly copied as Sundara. Surasundari
Nadiamsa extends from 8 deg. 48’ to 9 deg. of a
movable sign; from 21 deg. to 21 deg. 12’ of an
immovable sign; and from 23 deg. 48’ to 24 deg.
of a common sign.

1590-1592. Rahu’s period: If the Moon
rules the 7th house and is placed in the 10th
house, the native will gain wealth through a base
king in the dasa periods of Rahu. He will further
acquire male issues during the said period.
Should Rahu be in the 8th/10th house with one of
the three Navamsaas, viz. Pisces, Libra and Virga,
the subject in Rahu dasa will visit pilgrim centers
and temples on festive occasions. In the end of
this sub-period, he will incur physical illnesses
and enmity with friends and close relatives.
Suitable propitiations be done (to surmount the
evils concerned).

cohabiting with women given to anger.

1395-1596. Luminaries’ periods: If the Sun
and the Moon exchange their houses for a
Capricorn birth, they will in their dasa and subperiods (either way) cause danger. In the end of

may even die. If he survives, he will (with this
exchange) not cross 61 years of age, but will acquire pleasures and fortunes.

apply to a movable sign assurging.

On Progeny
पत्रलग्नेशदरिशगुरूभिः 1.
TEST प्रजातस्य सन्तानप्रतिबन्धकम्।
पत्ररूढाधिपे शुक्रे लग्नात् कर्मत्रिकोणगे। । १७७६ । |
FAR प्रजातः सः सन्तानप्रतिबन्धवान्।
. 1775-17761/2, Assessment of native’s son
(1.6. progeny) be done through the following: The
lords of 5th, ascendant and the 7th apart from
Jupiter. One born in Pankaja Nadiamsa will have
obstacles in obtaining progeny if the lords of the
5th and 7th are in debilitation. If Venus becomes
the lord of the Putra Aroodha Pada (i.e. Aroodha
pada for the Sth house) and is placed in the 10th
or its trine, counted from rising sign, the same
result will materialise. This will apply only when
the rising sign’s Navamsa falls in Pisces.
Notes: For Pankaja Nadiamsa, please see
notes under sloka 685- 686. From sloka 1775 to
1778, Pankaja Nadiamsa, Capricorn ascendant is
dwelt with.
(a) If Venus and Moon are in simultaneous
debility, the native in question will have obstacles
in begetting progeny.
(b) Venus is lord of 5th. He should also
become lord of 5th Aroodha or Putraroodha.
Being so he must occupy Libra, or Aquarius, or
Gemini to give same effects as at "a" above.
As for condition ‘b’, the Navamsa ascendant
should be in Pisces. This is not possible as
Pankaja Nadiamsa in Capricorn ascendant comes
to Vrigo Navamsa.
This should be interepreted as Venus fulfilling the conditions stated above should 218० be in
Pisces Navamsa - his exaltation Navamsa, to create obstacles in respect of progeny.
This view is supported by Venus being in 5th
house and being in Pisces Navamsa & C bringing
in the picture a great delay in birth of children or
through second marriage, vide sloka 1779-1780
infra,
Apparently this strength of Venus is a discount for progeny in case of Capricorn ascendant.

1793-1794, Moon in Indra Nadiamsa: In
case of birth in the former half of Champaka
Nadiamsa, the Moon (being ascendant lord)
placed in Indra Nadiamsa will make the native
beautiful, intelligent, skilful in speech and bestow
upon him charming face and eyes. He will be

obtain Rajayoga. He will have blood-red complexion. Of medium build, he will look short-statured.

1795-1801. Venusian Trimsamsa: Birth in
Cancer Lagna, former half of Champaka
Nadiamsa, in the captioned Trimsamsa will give
following results. The native will be fond of sagely
persons. He will honour gods and Brahmins. He
will eat limited food. He will eat less, sleep less
and be extremely cautious. He will be skillful in
understanding the thoughts of others. He will be
fond of ornaments, and be himself an ornament
(of the family), He will be wicked amongst wicked people; his acts will be evil in nature when it
concerns the evil. He will be bereft of paternal
fortunes. He will enjoy wealth earned by his own
labour. He will be an expert in Sastras relating
to Dharma (1.6. Scriptures dealing with Religion)
if the 4th lord Venus is in 9th in Navamsa. He will
Chandra Kala Nadi
be helpful to all. He will be endowed with
prowess, adventure and fame. He will be fond of
listening to mythological stories. Being rich, he
will be well disposed to poor persons. He will fast
to obtain the mercy of Lord Siva. He will be a
devotee of Lord Vishnu and Lord Siva. He will
follow virtuous path and possess wealth obtained
through very fair means. He will have fortunes in
agriculture and cow-breeding. He will lord over
villages and lands.
Notes: Champaka Nadiamsa in Cancer
does not occupy the degree of Venus’ Trimsamsa.
In such cases we have only to select the trimsamsa
condition.

1809. The native of Champaka Nadiamsa

जीवे कुजयुते दृष्टे योगश्चामरसंज्ञकः।
चामरे योगजातस्य SAT, पर् सुखम् १८१०॥
भेरीमृदंगवाद्यादि चम्पकांशे तु जायते।
1810-18101/2. Chamara Yoga: Jupiter being
in the company of or being in aspect to Mars
causes Chamara Yoga. The subject of this yoga
will get prosperity after 32 years of age. (Bheri
Mridanga Vadyaadi etc. denote musical drums
etc. which are not added in the translation as they
have no relevance here.)
Notes: The kind of Chamara Yoga discussed in the text relates to lords of the 9th and
10th for Cancer ascendant. In effect, the subject
rises in life after 32.
Chamara Yoga is explained in three different ways by standard texts. These are as under:
a) Lord of ascendant exalted and placed in
an angle in aspect to Jupiter.
b) Two benefics joining in one of the four
places, viz. ascendant, 9th. 10th and 7th.
Results for either of the above two kinds of
Chamara Yoga are: The native will be a king, be
honoured by a king, be a scholar, eloquent and
long-lived.
c) Ascendant occupied by a benefic while its
lord is in exaltation. Results: long-life, prosperity,
fame, virtues.

1811-1812. Kendraadi Yoga: The Sun and
other (six planets occupying (exclusively) the four
houses, viz. 11th, 9th, 8th and 7th will produce a
yoga called Kendraadi Yoga denoting fame and
happiness after 25 years of age. One born in Adhi
Yoga will be famous, wealthy, great and be endowed with horses, palanquins and other kinds of
conveyances, and the name and fame of his
spouse and progeny will ever shine like the Moon.
He will be endowed with fame, valour and adventure. He will attain fame in a foreign land.
Notes: The first line of sloka 1811 is a partial
presentation which specifies musical instruments
like Bheri, Mridanga etc.
These two slokas deal with two different
yogas, Kendradi Yoga and Adhi Yoga.
The condition laid down for Kendradi yoga
along with its effects are clear.

Adhi Yoga effects are mentioned, without
giving rules of formation. The reader is referred
to standard texts like Parasara Hora, Jataka
Parijata, etc.

1813-1815. Kahala Yoga: For a Cancer
Lagna native, Mars in Vargothama Navamsa in
Capricorn, being in the company of Saturn will
produce Kahala Yoga. As a result, the native will
possess abundant grains, much wealth, scented
articles, garlands and ornaments, and be happy.
He will be blessed with the splendour of the sun.
He will be highly intelligent and radiant.
Valorous, given to anger, firm in disposition, adventurous etc. are additonal effects. He will be
enthusiastic among the four kinds of armies
(using men, horses, elephants and chariots) and
wil exhibit ilimitable valour in times of war.

In the present slokas, another variety of
Kahala Yoga is discussed involving thé lords of
7th and Sth-cum-10th for Cancer ascendant.

सुधांशे त्वचे लग्ने कन्यारूढे प्रजायते।
शूद्रजन्मा सुखी नित्यं पंचमोअयं प्रकीर्तितः।। १८२४॥
अथवा षष्टपुत्रस्तु पूर्वभागेन संयुते।
उत्तरांशे प्रजातः स सर्वषु प्रथमःस्मृतः॥ १८२५॥
1824-1825. Sudha Nadiamsa: One born in
Sudha Nadiamsa of an immovable ascendant,
having its Aroodha in Virgo, will be aSoodra (4th
caste in Vedic terms), be ever happy and will be
the 5th issue of his parents. Or he may be the 6th
inorder. These effects are for birth in the former
half of he Nadiamsa in question. If birth be in
.ae second half, he will be the eldest issue.
Notes: Sudha Nadiamsa ranges thus: From
18 deg. 36’ to 18 deg. 48’ of a movable sign; from
11060. 12’ tolldeg. 24’ of an immovable sign; and
from 3 deg. 36’ to 3 deg. 48’ of a common sign.
This part of the text discusses Sudha
Nadiamsa. Sudha means nectar, lime. Here the
ascendant should be an immovable one. Occasionally Taurus features in particular.
Vide sloka 1824, the ascendant’s Aroodha
should fall in Virgo. For Leo/Aquarius, the
Aroodha ascendant cannot be Virgo. For Taurus,
Venus in Cancer yields Virgo as Aroodha Lagna.
Similarly for Scorpio, Mars in Aries takes Aroodha Lagna to Virgo. Thus by implication, this
particular rule relates only to Taurus and Scorpio
ascendants, and not to the other two immovable
ascendants.

1826-1827. Second Half - Sudha Nadiamsa:
Birth in this part of the Zodiac (in an immovable
ascendant) will be near a temple of Lord Siva
adjoining a river. The street in question will be
spread from east to west (and the house) facing
the north. It will be a city.

1828-1830. First Half - Seethala Nadiamsa:
If birth be in the first half of Seethala Nadiamsa,
in Taurus ascendant, with Jupiter therein, the
native will be endowed with longevity. He will
possess a symmetric body. For a native of first
half (of the said Nadiamsa), results due for
Jupiter (occupying the ascendant) will materialize in full measure. At the time of his birth, the
mother would incur much labour pains. But his
coming into this world will be instant (without any
anxiety). Because of Jupiter being seated in the
rising sign, the child (and the mother) will be
happy and be devoid of infantile evils. In case of
second half, infantile mortality will certainly come
to pass.
Notes: Jupiter in Taurus ascendant which
has Seethala Nadiamsa’s firsthalf will undoubtedly endow the native with full life-span which will
not be discounted by any other adverse yogas.

lot
For the native of second hlaf, the life-span can be
somewhat affected even though Jupiter resides in
the ascendant. The text clearly states that
childhood will see danger to life-span which can
however be overcome.
Other effects relating to mother etc. apply
equally to both the halves of the Nadiamsa.

half of Seethala Nadiamsa, see whether Venus in

brother nor a younger brother. He will have two
sisters endowed with longevity. In case of second
half of Seethala Nadiamsa, two sisters will be
long-lived (irrespective of Venus & C).

will have no male coborn - older or younger.

1855-1858, Obstacles: For birth in an immovable ascendant, see if Sth lord Mercury, being
in the company of Venus, is placed in the ending
part of Libra. In that case, the spouse of the
native will have obstacles in giving birth to a child.
She will have some abortions as well, and consequent health troubles (as the term "Aathuraa"
explains). Saturn in the 5th house, being in the
company of Mars, if found in Gemini Navamsa
(or one of the other three dual Navamsas) the
native will find it difficult to beget progeny. However, Mercury in addition should be in a degree
on which he is stationary (before/after retrogression/direction). To overcome this blemish, childsacrifice be done. (For obvious reasons, this
remedy should be flatly ignored.) Then the native
will be blessed with two long-living sons and longliving daughter.

1860-1863. Former half - Seethala Nadiamsa: The native of this degree ascending in Taurus
will be a worshipper of Lord Vishnu. He will
administer rulership of some colonies. Sometimes he will follow the codes prescribed by
Smarithi, (or traditional-laws recorded by memory
as against Sruthi, laws directly heard). He will
prosper after his 25th year. He will have selfearned wealth. He will make friends with men in
royal service, and will gain conveyances and
robes. His prosperity will be obstructed and he
will be mentally distressed when Saturn in transit
comes to the 8th sign counted from the natal
Moon’s Navamsa, (or) in Rahu’s sub period(s) (in
general).

1866-1870. Dasa Periods: The Dasa results
being explained now apply to birth in first half of

Deva Keralam 167
9661818 Nadiamsa. His third dasa will bring

1871-1872. The Luminaries: For a Seethala
Nadiamsa native, the Sun in Karala Shashtiamsa
and the Moon in Nirmala Nadiamsa will ensure a
balance between merits and sins (good and bad
effects). During his mortal journey, he will live a

Notes: The Sun should be in Karala Shashtiamsa which extends from 23 deg. 30’ to 24 deg.

meaning ‘frightful-teethed’. The planet Moon
should be in Nirmala Nadiamsa, for which please
refer to notes under sloka 847 supra.

1949-19521/2. Lords of 6th & 12th: Now
about the effects of the lords of the 6th and 12th.
The subject will be troubled by a great disease if
the 6th Aroodha Pada is aspected by ascendant
lord or by Saturn, 25106 10th lord is endowed with
strength. With other major transits being evil and
with the Sun in adverse Rasis, during the currency

114 Chandra Kala Nadi
of the dasa of the 12th lord, the subject will be
inflicted by a serious illness. If he survives, his
wealth will be destroyed. In the sub periods ruled
by favourable planets, the 12th lord’s dasa will
bestow wealth. Depending on favourabie planets
in transit, progress of the native will be on the
increase. Gains through progeny will be more,
and fortunes(?) less. He will be greatly grieved
due to loss of progeny.
Notes: Lords of 6th and 12th respectively
are Mars and Venus. For Scorpio ascendant, if
the Aroodha for the 6th is aspected by Saturn or
by Mars, the native will invite dangerous diseases.
10th lord Sun’s strength (not weakness) is a precondition here.
With such a yoga, the dasa of 12th lord Venus
is said to cause either a serious illness, or destruction of wealth.
Wealth will however be earned in the
favourable sub periods c: Venus dasa. For Scorpio ascendant, favourable planets in general are
Jupiter, the Sun, the Moon and Mars. These four
sub periods in the dasa of Venus will give wealth.
Venus dasa is also said to be a minus point
in respect of progeny.

1964-1965. Sub-periods of Mars and of
Mercury: Inthe dasa ruled by the 6th lord (Mars)
and in the sub-periods of 6th lord (Mars) and of
8th lord (Mercury), the native’s health will
decline due to his kinsfolk being in adversities.

ruled by the lords of the various ascendants
caused by the Shadvargas (six divisions) of the
natal ascendant.
Notes: Shadvargas are Rasi, Hora, Navamsa, Drekkana, Dvadasamsa and Trimsamsa. The
week-days denoted by the lords of these 6 ascendants will not prove harmful in the present context. The weekdays other than these will cause
illness to the native during the rulership of Mercury sub period and Mars sub period - both in the
dasa ruled by 6th lord Mars.

1970-1971. Strength of the Moon & Ruling
Asterism: On the day when the Moon is strong
and the ruling asterism is favourable, the native
will be happy with his wife, will eat food of good
quality, which is sweet and specially. liked by the
native, On these days, plenty of grains will exist
in his home. On such days when the Moon is not
strong, and the ruling asterism is adverse, the
subject will have to commit some sinful acts, wil
have lack of sleep, be distressed and will be weak
in arguments.

1978. Death of Son: Should Saturn be in the
9th in the company of Mars, while the significator
for progeny, Jupiter, is with Rahu, the native will
incur loss of a son. To overcome this grief,
remedies as prescribed by Sastras be resorted to.

Deva Keralam
Notes: Saturn-Mars in the 9th while Jupiter
is with Rahu will cause death of a son. However,
if Saturn in the process obtains Leo Navamsa (i.e.
Saturn in Cancer between 3 deg. 20° and 6 deg.
40’) will not cause such a loss, but the child will be
long-lived (vide sloka 1983 infra).
In case Saturn is not in Leo Navamsa,
remedies as prescribed in slokas 1979-1982 infra
be resorted to.

1979-1982. To overcome such an evil (and to

son will be born (whol will not incur early death).

to a Brahmin on a Monday (Timmamsu = Moon,
who has rays that are not fierce). Worship of such
an image of the Sun will eliminate all diseases
(obstructing birth of a son) and instant results will
prevail, A son so born will be intelligent,
valorous, modest, and skilful in arts. Alternatively
a heavy image of a child be donated following
which wealth and acquisition of a child will occur.

उच्चनीचे usa स्वक्षत्रग्रहवर्चिते।
परस्परक्षेत्रयोगे महाराजो भवेन्नरः। | १९९४ |
1994, The conjunction of an exalted planet
with a debilitated planet, devoid of the company
of its dispositor, will be the cause of a Rajayoga.
An emperor is born if (two) planets exchange
their Rasis.
Notes: Mars and Jupiter can join in
Capricorn without Saturn’s position therein, or
they can join in Cancer without the Moon therein.
Similarly the Sun-Saturn conjunction in Aries
should not be shared by Mars in Aries. Nor
should the Sun-Saturn in Libra acquire Venusian
association.
Thus four Raja Yogas are produced by the
company of an exalted planet and a debilitated
planet.
The second yoga given here is wellknown
Parivarthana Yoga.

1997-1998. Ashtaka Varga: Firstly, commit
trinal reduction, then Ekadhipatya Sodhana
(reduction on account of a planet owning two
signs). Multiply the net dots by seven and divide

individual Ashtaka Varga.
After the two reductions, the bindus be multiplied by seven and multiples of eight be cast
away. The remainder 3 will give skill in speech
apart from a number of children.
Jupiter’s fatal degrees are:
Aries 19 deg.
Taurus 29 deg.
Gemini 21 deg.

Leo 6 deg.
Virgo 4 deg.
Libra 13 deg.
Scorpio 4 deg.
Sagit 27 deg.
Capri 12 deg.
Aqua 29 deg.
Pisces 19 deg.
These degrees applicable only to Jupiter
should be used in this particular context when the
Sth house is badly afflicted with other combinations required being in existence.
Particularly, the ‘fatal degree’ for Jupiter in
Cancer Sagittarius and Pisces should be handled
with care,

2026-2029. Nirmala Nadiamsa: During the
Sun’s northerly course, coinciding with his transit
in the three signs commencing Aries, the mother
of the subject of Nirmala Nadiamsa will be in
danger. In the dasa that is sixth in sequence, the
native of Nirmala Nadiamsa will incur physical
illness. This will occur in the end of the 4th dasa,
or in‘adverse years/months. One born in this
Nadiamsa in an immovable ascendant will experience royal wrath in the sub-period denoted
by fifth Nakshatra from the natal Moon, during
the rulership of 6th major period. To overcome
this, suitable propitiations be done. For a native
of Nirmala Nadiamsa, former half, the 6th dasa
will bring danger. If at all he survives, the dasa
ruled by the 8th lord will endanger him.
Notes: Slokas 2026 -2032 are wrongly appended here. These deal with Nirmala Nadiamsa
of an immovable ascendant. For details of Nirmala Nadiamsa, please see notes under sloka
79-80 1/2 supra. In this portion of the text, Nirmala and Champaka Nadiamsas are clubbed
together. The reader may carefully assort such
rules without confusion.

2030-2032. If the ascendant lord is in Vargothama, or be in exaltation, or be in aspect to
Jupiter, the native will follow Advaitha (Nonduality), will be devoted to Philosophy and will
have knowledge of the Supreme Being. If the Sun
is in Karala Shashtiamsa, the native will live in the
manner prescribed for his family, and be endowed with wisdom. Sukra Nadi deals with in
detail the Yoga aspects. The results for Lagna
Nadi (amsa) narrated in Deva Keralam will bear
, truth.

maleficence of the Sun and gives wisdom to the
native. `

2109-2110. Mercury in Jarjhara Nadiamsa:
(According to Budha Nadi) Mercury in Jarjhara
Nadiamsa for a birth in Vargothama ascendant
with the first quarter of Gada Nadiama will give
following effects. The subject will be the second

Notes: The present part deals with
Mercury’s position in Jarjhara Nadiamsa (also
known as Jarjhari) which spaces itself as under:
From 4 deg. to 4 deg. 12’ of a movable sign;
from 25 deg. 48’ to 26 deg. of an immovable sign;
and from 19 deg. to 19 deg. 12’ of a common sign.
The present yogas are from Budha Nadi.
In view of Vargothama in a common sign,
Gada Nadiamsa should be excluded. They cannot go together. Hence from slokas 2109-2111,
Gada Nadiamsa to ascend doés not apply.

2143-2146. Progeny: If the Moon, aspected
by Mars, is in extremis, and at the same time
aspects the Aroodha Lagna, the subject will face
hurdles in begetting progeny. One born in

Deva Keralam
Jagathi Nadiamsa will only obtain dead children.
After a long interval, through his second wife, he
will obtain progeny. He should recite
Mahamantra installing a deity’s image and read
Vishnu Sahsranama for a myriad times. Worship
as prescribed be conducted regularly. These
remedies will ward off numerous blemishes. And
after a great deal of efforts, he will obtain 2 female
issue. She will live long.
Notes: The Moon will be in extremis if she
is in the last six degrees of an odd sign, or in the
first six degrees of an even sign.
The Moon so placed should be aspected by
Mars. The Moon should aspect the Arroodha
ascendant. The result is hurdle in acquiring
progeny.
The word "Ayutha" in sloka 2145 means a
myriad or even ten thousand. Vishnu Sahasra
Nama should be recited for ten thousand times,
as a remedy to overcome obstacles in acquiring
progeny.
Another remedy suggested is recitation of
Mahamantra installing the image of a deity. The
deity concerned is not specified. Mahamanira is:
any very sacred or efficacious text of the Vedas,
particularly a great spell against a snake’s venom.

Deva Keralam
2166. Vaishnavite: The 5th lord in Kunda
Nadiamsa related to Saturn by aspect/company,
will be devoted to the Wheel of Lord Vishnu and
be interested in following the codes prescribed
for a Vaishnavite. He will be quite intelligent.
Notes: In ardent Vaishnava circles, a true
and enlightened devotee worships the Sudarsana
(the Wheel held by Lord Vishnu to destroy evils
in the Universe) and Sankha (the Conch called
Panchajanya, with which the God raises His voice
against evils). These two Symbols are held in
great reverence by the Vaishnavites.
In a nutshell, the sloka refers to the native
being a great devotee of Lord Vishnu. This apart,
the position of Sth lord, Mercury, with a connection with ascendant lord Saturn marks a piercing
intelligence on the part of the native.

When Saturn in transit comes to the Rasi represented by the Navamsa tenanted by the ascendant

2174-21751/2. Thread Ceremony & Wealth:
During the 2nd dasa, when Saturn traverses the
sign represented by the 8th lord’s Navamsa at
birth or its trine, the native’s Thread Ceremony
will take place. When Jupiter mov.ng in transit
attains Aries Navamsa, during the rulership of the
2nd dasa, the subject will beget wealth. Jupiter
traversing in Gemini will also increase one’s fortunes. The native’s coborns will also be happy
and prosperous. (These apply to birth in Kunda
Nadiamsa, of an immovable ascendant, particularly Aquarius.)

Notes: The 8th lord’s Navamsa, normally
used for death and such other tragic events is used
by the text in the context of Thread Ceremony (or
Upanayana). For immovable ascendants, Jupiter
and Mercury rule the 8th as the case may be.
These two planets are in charge of such Vedic
functions. Venus (another benefic) holds sway
over material functions like marriage.

or conjunct with Saturn. Short life is denoted if
the 8th lord counted from the Moon is with two
malefics without a benefic’s aspect.

2229-2235. There will be sometimes
Balarishta effects for a native of Mangala
Nadiamsa, former half, if Venus joins the ascendant lord. This will be during the "dasa about to
retrograde". (This expression is faulty). These
effects will come to pass in the end/beginning of
first dasa, or in the first sub period of second dasa.
Due to the past merits of the father, the child will
survive. Performance of Mrityunjaya Japa and
donation of an image of the zodiac will eliminate
Balarishta effects as a hill remains unaffected by

Deva Keralam
lightning. If the native is in the dasas of the
luminaries or of Rahu, prayers be performed,
clothes be donated and recitation of Mrityunjaya
Japa for a hundred times be done. Benefics in
angle/trines will confer a medium span of life.
One will be short-lived if birth is in the first onethird part of first half Mangala Nadiamsa; and be
long-lived if birth is in the middle portion of the
same Nadiamsa. The life-span will be full if birth
is in the first one-third part of second half of
Mangala Nadiamsa. Long life is denoted for a
subject of middle part of second half.
Notes: "Dasa about to retrograde” should
actually read as first dasa, as Balarishta always
occurs in the first dasa. If the same is however for
a short duration, then it can go tu second dasa,
but before the child is twelve. The text also supports this view.

2240. Jupiter’s aspect: See if Jupiter
aspects the 10th house in case of a Mangala
Nadiamsa native (of Aries ascendant). Riches
right from birth will exist, in such a case, if birth
is in the former part of the Nadiamsa in question.
However, if birth is in the 2nd half, only (perennial) poverty will result this yoga.
Notes: Jupiter’s aspect on Leo is welcome
for wealth if birth is in first half of Mangala
Nadiamsa in Aries. If the birth ascendant shifts
to second part of this Nadiamsa in Aries, the
aspect of Jupiter on Leo causes endless poverty.

this kind of yoga will cause obstacles to the

and 26 deg. 40’ of space.

2282-2287. The lord of the 9th in Varuna
Nadiamsa while the 5th lord is in Kumara
Nadiamsa denotes that the native was a Soodra in
the last birth and the next birth following the
present one will also be of a Soodra. In the
current birth he will be famous, be endowed with
great wealth, be passionate and happy. For one
born in Mangala Nadiamsa, the Sun in Karaala
Shashtiamsa while the 9th lord is in Varuna
Nadiamsa, good and bad effects will accrue in
equal measure. He will attain the heavens. According to some, he will go to some good world.
The 12th lord in Deva Shastiamsa will give wisdom in the end. The effects from Lagna Nadi
narrated in Deva Keralam will bear truth.

2305. Rahu in Aslesha Nakshatra: If Rahu
at birth is placed in Aslesha Nakshatra, he will
give following effects: The native will be endowed with wealth, be valorous, and be grateful.
If Rahu is in the company of another malefic and
is in a benefic’s navamsa, the subject will be
beautiful (Varna also means renown, property
etc.) and will eat anything. He will be ever given
to passion.
